To recover those numbers, the main result tables, ablation tables, and the appendix details on continuation count and support size H should be checked.48:T450,This is a skim paper rather than a line-by-line derivation paper. Start with the introduction and lock in the distinction between distillation compatibility and reasoning progress. Then jump to the method figure and the reward-filtering definition; the key question is why process reward is used as a validator instead of a new optimization term. After that, read the theoretical part only for the two claims that matter operationally: merged spans reduce boundary-noise sensitivity, and segment-level averaging reduces variance.

In the experiments, focus on three things: qualitative mismatch cases under standard OPD, comparisons against direct reward shaping or reward mixing, and ablations removing merging or changing the filtering granularity. Around 25 to 35 minutes should be enough to extract the main ideas.

The most transferable idea is that auxiliary supervision does not always need to enter the loss directly. It can be used to gate, filter, or calibrate another training signal. That pattern may carry over to synthetic data filtering, RL signal gating, and teacher-ensemble arbitration.49:T476,Re$^3$Cap treats multimodal retrieval as a semantic constraint mechanism.
1) Image-to-dataset retrieval: given an image v, retrieve Top-K similar image-text pairs R_v from a paired corpus D using an image retrieval model.
2) CRS (Caption Refinement Suggester): extract semantic elements that consistently appear across the retrieved captions; these act as “must-preserve / should-include” anchors.
3) CQA (Caption Quality Assessor): use the generated caption as a query to retrieve another set R_c; discrepancies between R_v and R_c are interpreted as hallucinations (caption introduces content that shifts retrieval away from the image-based neighborhood) or omissions (missing details that prevent alignment).
4) RL training uses CRS+CQA outputs to guide caption refinement, encouraging generation of new candidates rather than merely scoring existing ones.
Key differences vs common RL captioning: it avoids relying on a single VLM/CLIP scalar judge, does not require extra keyword annotations, and aims to reduce reward hacking by grounding feedback in retrieval-set consistency—assuming the retrieval model and corpus are reliable.4a:T485,The framework, Multi-Turn Certified Robustness (MTCR), models conversation as a State-Adversarial MDP. Dialogue history is mapped to a continuous state; at each turn an adversary chooses a user message from an epsilon-bounded perturbation set; the LLM responds according to its policy; and the state is updated. Safety is defined by a predicate over the state space. The central quantity is k-turn certified robustness: the worst-case probability of staying safe for k turns under any adaptive adversary. A naive certificate gives a lower bound of p^k, where p is the worst-case single-turn certified safety probability. MTCR tightens this through embedding-space mode decomposition, separately certifying intra-mode safety and inter-mode transitions. It also introduces (alpha, beta)-safety persistence, a structural condition under which the degradation can improve from p^k to beta^k when beta is larger than the worst-case single-turn bound, with a linear characterization 1 - k(1 - beta) for interpretable horizon estimates. The paper also discusses tightness for non-overlapping decompositions and impossibility results without structural assumptions.4b:T49c,The dataset contains 40 Korean conference sessions, 19 medical and 21 finance, with multilingual segments preserved when present. Each condition produces 2,598 to 2,765 QA items, a roughly 6% range. The main grid covers 9 models from five families, EXAONE, Gemma, Llama, Phi, and Qwen, ranging from 2.4B to 70B parameters. The main experiment has 72 LoRA runs, plus 12 full fine-tuning sanity runs and an 8-cell Whisper-medium STT-swap grid. The LoRA recipe uses rank 16, alpha 32, dropout 0.05, 4-bit QLoRA, learning rate 2e-4, 3% warmup, 3 epochs, and effective batch size 16. Evaluation includes KMMLU, KMMLU-Pro, MMLU, four LLM judges, six blind human experts, STT substitution, and an LLM difficulty audit. QA quality improves by +0.18 according to LLM judges and +0.22 according to humans on a 1–5 scale, but the cross-model mean MCQA gain is not significant. Gains concentrate in model-family and domain-aligned settings. Replacing the internal STT system with Whisper-medium changes aggregate KMMLU by at most 1.32 percentage points on average. The difficulty audit finds that two strong LLM judges still cannot answer 7.8% of the public-sample QA items with confidence.4c:T7ca,The setup is built around Qwen3-8B as a hybrid model. The same base model can be decoded in think mode, where it generates reasoning before deciding, or in no-think mode, where it emits a direct action token. This allows a relatively controlled comparison between a reasoning policy and a direct classifier without changing the model family. The task is binary intervention timing: given a prefix of a multi-party conversation, decide whether an assistant should speak now or remain silent. The dataset contains about 16,000 synthetic conversations expanded into roughly 173,000 token-level decision points, with only about 13% positive intervention labels, so the class imbalance is severe. The paper compares several training routes. One is direct decision-token SFT, which tends to produce a stronger classifier but suppresses reasoning. Another is a masked objective that preserves the model’s native reasoning, but only on cases the model already solves correctly; this means it receives little useful signal on failure cases and therefore struggles to improve beyond the base reasoning policy. A third route is think-mode RL, optimizing either accuracy or a behavioral faithfulness reward. The authors point out a key limitation of group-relative RL objectives: when all sampled rollouts agree, there is no learning signal, even if they all agree on the wrong answer. That leaves many confidently wrong prompts untouched. For auditing, the paper combines activation probes with behavioral interventions. Probes test when the decision becomes decodable in the hidden states, while interventions remove or replace parts of the generated reasoning to see whether the final action changes, which is a more causal test of whether reasoning matters. The paper also argues that several common metrics are misleading in this setting, including probability-based contribution scores, probe accuracy that ignores class imbalance, probing only at the end of reasoning, and uncontrolled flip tests.4d:T613,The main empirical result is that the strongest action policy is the direct classifier that does not explicitly generate reasoning, while the reasoning policy is more auditable but less accurate, with the largest cost coming from missed interventions. That is the central capability–auditability tradeoff. In training experiments, decision-token SFT further strengthens the direct policy but pushes reasoning toward irrelevance. The masked objective cannot substantially beat the base reasoning policy because it only preserves reasoning on examples the model already gets right. Think-mode RL also fails to clearly surpass the base reasoning policy, not because RL is useless in general, but because the group-relative objective gives no gradient on prompts where all sampled rollouts agree, including confidently wrong ones. The audit results are also informative. For the reasoning policy, the eventual decision becomes decodable during the chain of thought, whereas for the direct classifier the decision is already decodable at the prompt stage. That supports the interpretation that the direct policy commits early, while the reasoning policy at least partially forms the decision during reasoning. The authors also stress that single-run comparisons can confuse seed variance with reward-objective effects, so multiple seeds or replicates are necessary when comparing RL variants. The excerpt provided here does not include the full numeric tables, but the paper is clearly organized around mechanism evidence rather than a single headline score.4e:T588,The architecture has three parts: a frozen backbone LLM, a safety-specific LoRA adapter, and a learned gate that outputs a continuous routing score g in [0,1] from hidden-state representations. The adapter is not simply turned on or off. Its strength is scaled continuously inside the model, with the effective transformation written as h' = (W + gΔW)h. This makes the method closer to interpolation between the base model and a safety-adapted model, rather than hard switching or post-hoc filtering.

A key design choice is that the gate is trained jointly with the safety adapter. It is not an external moderation classifier. The gate directly modulates internal computation during generation. The paper adds two training ingredients to make routing discriminative. First, subtype-aware gate weighting gives different emphasis to different harmful and benign prompt categories, aiming to reduce false positives on safety-adjacent benign inputs. Second, a hard pairwise margin objective explicitly separates harmful and benign prompts in gating space. This is important because plain generation loss may not force a clean routing boundary. Compared with standard safety LoRA, the novelty is not the adapter itself but the input-conditioned continuous activation. Compared with guard-model pipelines, the novelty is that routing happens inside the model’s latent computation rather than as an external pre-filter.4f:T755,The evaluation covers safety with HarmBench and XSTest, and utility with GSM8K, MMLU, and TruthfulQA, using Llama-3-8B-Instruct and Gemma-2-2B-it as the main backbones. The paper’s case is not built on one isolated metric. It is built on a better trade-off point.

On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reportedly drives HarmBench attack success rate close to zero. Against existing aligned variants in the Llama-3 and Alpaca families, CLEAR reaches 0.50% ASR, while the compared aligned variants remain around 15.50% to 24.00%. At the same time, it retains 73.46% on GSM8K, about 7 points higher than globally applied SFT and standard LoRA. That is the most important result in the paper because it suggests the utility gain is not coming from weaker safety, but from selective intervention.

On Gemma-2-2B-it, the same pattern appears and is arguably more informative for smaller models: GSM8K improves from roughly 38% under SFT/LoRA to 42%, and TruthfulQA MC2 rises from about 47% to 53%. This supports the idea that smaller models suffer more from global safety tuning and therefore benefit more from conditional routing.

XSTest is used to probe routing quality rather than end-task generation alone. The hidden-state gate, with fewer than 1M parameters, is claimed to achieve a favorable harmful-versus-benign routing trade-off. That matters because it distinguishes CLEAR from simply adding another classifier in front of the model.

The most important ablations to inspect are the comparisons against always-on LoRA, removal of the pairwise margin objective, and removal of subtype-aware weighting. The central question is whether utility preservation truly comes from conditional routing rather than from a weaker safety adapter. If the paper shows CLEAR maintaining similar or better ASR while preserving noticeably higher GSM8K or TruthfulQA, then the argument is strong.50:T412,Read it as a routing paper, not as another safety fine-tuning paper. Start with the method figure and the equation for gated adapter injection to verify that the gate modulates internal LoRA strength rather than acting as an output filter. Then read the training objective carefully, especially the subtype-aware weighting and pairwise margin terms, because those are the pieces that make the gate more than a soft classifier. Finally, inspect the main tables where HarmBench is shown alongside GSM8K and TruthfulQA. The key question is whether CLEAR preserves utility at comparable safety, not whether it simply relaxes refusal behavior.

If time is limited, 20 to 30 minutes is enough for a solid skim: Introduction for the alignment-tax framing, method section for the gate and losses, and the main result tables plus XSTest analysis. Two details are worth deeper inspection if available in the full text: which hidden states feed the gate, and whether the routing score is prompt-level, layer-specific, or token-dynamic during generation.51:T616,The central move is to treat interpretability as a measurement-design problem. The unknown \(x\) can be an effect vector, a local gradient map, or an interaction matrix, depending on the chosen basis and intervention family. Each row of \(A\) corresponds to an intervention, \(\tilde y\) is the observed response, and \(w\) collects what the linearized measurement model misses. Importantly, the framework does not force all methods to estimate the same target; instead, it provides a shared language for how measurements are obtained and how their errors should be analyzed.

The paper then studies when such measurements are useful. It analyzes recoverability conditions, the role of intervention scale and mask density, and the point at which first-order local measurements must be replaced or augmented by interaction-aware ones. A key concept is calibration dimension: the smallest declared correction family needed so that a baseline map can predict finite-scale held-out interventions. If a low-dimensional correction suffices, a local map may still be operationally useful after calibration. If not, the residual indicates missing interactions, a poor basis, or an inadequate measurement family.

Another important concept is the observer: an estimate that is meant to guide interventions. The paper argues that an observer should be judged not by whether it reconstructs an appealing internal story, but by whether it predicts intervention effects at the scale where control will actually be applied. This links interpretability directly to control.52:T45b,The experiments are designed to validate the framework rather than to win a benchmark. The most informative quantitative result is in the finite-scale correction setting: at a larger intervention scale, a one-dimensional fitted gain improves mean held-out attribution-patching \(R^2\) from 0.818 to 0.960, close to sparse recovery at 0.969. This supports the paper’s claim that some local maps are not fundamentally wrong, but need an explicitly declared finite-scale correction.

A second key result comes from the two-HMM wind-tunnel control experiment. With controller and actuator fixed, closed-loop target error tracks observer error across ten observer variants with Spearman correlation 0.95. This shows that interpretability error is not merely descriptive; it propagates into control error. The specificity test adds an important caveat: improving the target state alone is not enough, because an observer can still induce larger movement in a nuisance state. The excerpt provided does not include the full tables, so it is better to stick to these reported numbers rather than invent additional details.53:T43d,For a skim-level read, focus on three parts. First, read the introduction and Table 1 to understand how coordinate patching, attribution patching, subset recovery, and HVPs are unified under one measurement language. Second, read Section 2 and especially 2.1 for the error decomposition, recoverability conditions, and the definition of calibration dimension. Third, read Section 4 and Section 5.2 for the control-facing validation and the clearest numerical example of finite-scale correction. Expected reading time is about 30–40 minutes.

If your interests are closer to mechanistic analysis of LLMs, two issues are worth deeper attention. One is basis choice: the framework starts after a basis is proposed, but whether the target is sparse or recoverable depends heavily on whether the basis is heads, neurons, residual directions, or learned features. The other is intervention scale: many attribution methods are valid only infinitesimally, while practical steering and editing use finite interventions. The paper’s held-out intervention tests are aimed exactly at that gap.54:T55b,The method is a deterministic short-window probe. Starting from the last two tokens, the model predicts the next token by argmax; the window is rolled forward and the process is iterated, producing a discrete dynamical system. From 96 random two-token starts, the authors measure the fixed-point fraction φ, the proportion of trajectories that terminate in a fixed point. They also classify trajectory geometry into funnel, none, fragmented, and borderline, which gives a coarse structural summary of the attractor landscape. Prefixes are added as wrappers to every forward pass without changing the estimator, so the observed effect is attributed to the conditional distribution rather than to the readout definition. The paper compares raw, BOS, and ordinary text prefixes across six models and sweeps window size. The interesting part is that the paper is organized around failed factorizations. Prefix length is not a monotone driver. Two proposed content-side factors fail after widening the sample. Model-side hypotheses such as a universal direction, bidirectionality as a model property, and instruct-resistance also fail. Even the nearest mechanistic account, attention-sink strength, matches the sign of the fixed-point shift in only 2 of 5 models. What remains is pairwise and model-conditioned: the same prefix can push four models toward 0 and two toward 1.55:T61a,Training is modeled as a deterministic discrete-time dynamical system over a complete state S_t=(w_t,m_t,v_t,\tau_t), where w are parameters, m the first moment (momentum/Adam first-moment buffer), v the second moment, and \tau auxiliary deterministic state (clock/schedule).
- Forward sensitivity (transport): define q_t=\partial S_t/\partial \alpha for a source-strength parameter \alpha. The recurrence includes (a) injected source perturbations d_t (teacher-induced gradient changes) and (b) feedback through \partial G/\partial S because past perturbations alter w_t and therefore future gradients.
- Backward sensitivity (valuation): define a terminal observer O (in LLMs, a target-minus-reference candidate-string log-likelihood on a frozen prompt bank). Compute the costate p_T=(\partial O/\partial S_T)^\top and propagate it backward to obtain a per-step “future value” \lambda_t capturing how valuable a state perturbation at time t is for the final behavior.
- Exact transport–valuation identity: total behavioral change is expressed as a path integral pairing forward-propagated perturbations with backward-propagated values, making route dependence explicit.
- State surgery for causal identification: by transplanting components of the trainer state at a cut, they show the first moment m is the main carrier that continues to write source ancestry into weights after source removal. Transplanting m alone can change future trajectories while keeping current parameters/outputs unchanged; recovering terminal behavior requires (w,m) together.56:T492,CARD has three steps. First, for each axis such as belief, intent, or knowledge, the authors train L2-regularized logistic probes on per-layer, per-head activations at the final input token. Each example contains a matched statement and its negation, and the learned unit vector points from the negated statement toward the matched one. Second, they select the top K=56 layer-head pairs by held-out probe accuracy, roughly 7% of all heads, and use forward hooks to add a scaled version of the probe direction only at the final-token position and only in those head slices. The steering scale is alpha=+10 or alpha=-10 in units of the activation standard deviation along the probe direction. Third, they measure a different prediction axis after steering, for example steering belief while evaluating action. The main statistics are the true-belief versus false-belief accuracy gap shift and a mean-accuracy shift. The accompanying Relay Chain benchmark is a cooperative grid-world setup with belief, knowledge, intent, and action questions, plus success and counter partitions that separate constant-prior behavior, world-state tracking, and belief-conditional behavior.57:T584,The work has two parts. First, a diagnostic analysis. On four ordinal benchmarks and four MLLM backbones, the authors probe frozen hidden states across layers and prompt protocols to test whether ordered labels are linearly recoverable. They then go beyond probe scores and analyze how the unembedding matrix filters the ordinal direction, and how much of that direction is retained by the digit-token row subspace W_S. This turns the gap between probe-readability and generation into a concrete output-subspace issue. Second, they propose Ordinal Lens Alignment (OLA), a frozen-backbone inference-time correction method. OLA does not modify the vision encoder, LM backbone, or original unembedding. It trains lightweight lenses on K mid-to-deep decoder layers, with the lenses anchored to the digit-token row space, produces layer-wise ordinal distributions, fuses them with learned softmax weights into an instance-specific P_OLA, and then applies a discrepancy-driven correction to logits only at the answer position and only over the target digit-token set. The design choices are deliberate: multi-layer lenses because ordinal structure emerges across depth and varies by instance; W_S anchoring because the goal is to reconnect latent evidence to the native output interface rather than to maximize free probe accuracy; and target-restricted correction to avoid disturbing the full vocabulary distribution.58:T491,The strongest evidence is in the diagnostic section. Under the most informative setup, task-designed prompt plus last-token hidden state, all backbone-dataset combinations achieve probe SRCC of at least 0.756, with peaks including 0.938 on Adience with Qwen3-VL, 0.919 on Aesthetic with Gemma-4, and 0.843 on HCI with Qwen3-VL. In contrast, the digit-token row space retains less than 1.15% of the ordinal-direction energy across all 16 model-dataset pairs, which strongly supports the output-bottleneck claim. At the behavioral level, there is a 16 to 77 absolute-point accuracy gap between hidden-state linear probes and native digit-token outputs. That is large enough to argue that the main issue is not the absence of latent ordinal evidence. In method comparisons, OLA outperforms prompt-only, hidden steering, capacity-matched baselines, and offline-lens variants, and beats the LoRA-tuned OrderChain baseline in most settings while keeping the backbone frozen. For a closer read, the most useful checks are whether ACC, MAE, and SRCC improve consistently, and how much each component matters: number of layers K, W_S anchoring, and target-restricted correction.59:T4ec,The paper proposes and documents the Salt method as a disciplined workflow rather than a new learning algorithm.
- Fixed deliverable bundle per iteration: (i) implementation, (ii) formal specification, (iii) kernel-checked proof that the implementation meets the spec, (iv) tests including adversarial controls to ensure the proof has “bite,” and (v) certificates—simplified restatements of the spec designed for human comprehension.
- The key contract is implication: the kernel proves the formal statement entails the certificate statement, so the certificate can only be weaker than what was proved. This makes statement-level human review tractable without trusting the full formal development.
- Emphasis on specification risk: correctness is meaningless if the spec is wrong; therefore the workflow centers on active interrogation, where the human queries the system and receives kernel-checked evidence (unfolding binders, justifying hypotheses, producing variants under changed assumptions).
- End-to-end verified stack culminating in a RISC-V tapeout, with strict provenance (commit-verified theorem trails) and append-only error logs.
The design choice is to optimize for controllable autonomy at AI speed, not for minimal formalization effort.5a:T73a,CAMA has two main components: correlation-aware evidence decoupling and missing-evidence recovery.

The first component is query-conditioned latent-source arbitration. Instead of treating each retrieved memory as an independent vote, the method maps memories into latent evidential factors or slots. The intuition is that multiple memories may express the same underlying source of evidence, just through different agents, timestamps, or phrasings. CAMA uses neural dependency inference to estimate whether memories are redundant under the current query, and combines this with provenance-based symbolic priors derived from source chains, dependency links, or write traces. Hypothesis support is then aggregated at the latent-factor level rather than the raw-memory level, reducing repeated counting of the same evidence.

The second component is sequential evidence recovery. The paper argues that the initial retrieval set is itself biased: similarity-based retrieval tends to over-select correlated memories and miss independent evidence that could overturn a false majority. CAMA therefore does not restrict arbitration to the initial top-K slice. Instead, it learns a recovery policy that, under a budget, decides whether to expand retrieval or trace provenance/dependency links to acquire additional independent evidence. This is a sensible design because it treats the current evidence set as a biased sample rather than a complete basis for decision-making.

Methodologically, this is not a new LLM architecture. It is a combination of evidence aggregation, symbolic provenance, and sequential retrieval policy for multi-agent memory arbitration. The main novelty is making dependence modeling query-conditioned and latent-source based, rather than relying on static heuristics such as agent identity, timestamp, or semantic similarity alone.5b:T4fe,The experiments are designed to show that CAMA suppresses false majorities caused by correlated memories and outperforms standard baselines on long-term multi-agent memory benchmarks. The baseline families appear to include majority voting, confidence or relevance weighting, and retrieval-augmented reasoning methods. The qualitative result is straightforward: when memories share sources or propagate through the system, conventional aggregation overestimates support for a hypothesis, while CAMA is better able to recover the correct arbitration by grouping correlated evidence and recovering missing independent evidence.

This preserves the temporal structure of the update rule (e.g., per-token learning rate, momentum, decay) discarded by prior chunk-wise methods, while enabling full chunk-level hardware parallelism. Among many variants, score-entropy discrete diffusion (SEDD) has achieved particularly strong empirical results. In SEDD, new samples are generated by iteratively evaluating a sequence of concrete score functions, which are learned by minimizing a score-entropy loss.   While much of the prior theoretical literature on discrete diffusion has focused","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T00:16:00Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20635.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["stat.ML","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究 SEDD 中 concrete score 估计的统计极限：给定有限训练样本，学习离散扩散反向过程所需的密度比 p_t(y)/p_t(x) 到底需要多少样本，score-entropy loss 下最好的可能误差是多少。它把问题从“采样器在 score 足够准时是否收敛”推进到“score 本身在有限样本下能否被可靠估计”。","importance":"这是离散 diffusion LM 理论中比较核心的一块。SEDD、masked diffusion LM、LLaDA 一类非自回归语言建模方法，最终都要面对离散状态上的 denoising / score estimation 问题。已有理论更多分析初始化误差和离散化误差，通常把 score estimation error 当作给定条件。这里直接给出 minimax 下界，并构造接近该下界的估计器，说明 score-entropy 目标的统计代价来自哪里。对预训练研究的启发是：离散扩散不只受采样步数限制，也受 token 分布稀疏性、transition graph、时间噪声分布和密度比估计难度限制。语言数据上的长尾 token 和稀有上下文会使这个问题更尖锐。","technical_approach":"论文以 CTMC 离散扩散为框架。前向过程由 transition rate matrix 定义，在每个时间 t 诱导分布 p_t。反向过程需要 concrete score，也就是所有状态对上的密度比 p_t(y)/p_t(x)。SEDD 用 score-entropy loss 学这个比值：对样本 x 和候选跳转 y，用由 transition rate 决定的权重 w_yx 加权 Bregman divergence D(a,b)=a/b-1-log(a/b)，其中 b 是真实密度比，a 是模型估计。\n\n核心技术贡献是把 concrete score estimation 作为一个统计估计问题处理。先分析单个分布 p 下的 score 函数估计，给出 minimax lower bound；再给出一个简单估计器，通常可理解为基于经验频率的 plug-in 估计，并配合平滑或截断来避免低概率状态上的比值爆炸。上界与下界在主要项上匹配，可能只差常数或对数因子。随后把单时刻结果接到 SEDD 的时间依赖情形，用加权积分形式控制 E_SE，也就是总 KL 误差分解中的 score estimation 项。这个路线和研究 τ-leaping、uniformization、Gillespie、Euler 等采样器误差的工作互补：那些工作问“已知 score 后怎么采样更准”，这里问“有限样本能把 score 学到多准”。","experiments":"这是理论论文，没有常规 LLM 训练实验，也没有模型参数量、语料规模或 benchmark 性能数字。关键结果是定理而不是实验曲线。全文围绕三类误差展开：初始化误差 E_init 通常随时间 horizon T 指数衰减；离散化误差 E_dis 由采样算法和步长决定；score estimation error E_SE 由有限样本学习 concrete score 带来。论文的主要结果是给出 E_SE 相关的 minimax 下界，并证明一个简单估计器可近似达到该界。消融意义上的洞察是：当 p_t(x) 很小或状态空间/transition neighborhood 变大时，密度比估计会变难；这说明离散 diffusion LM 的统计瓶颈可能集中在低概率 token、长尾状态和高连通跳转结构上。","reading_guide":"建议精读，预计 1.5–2 小时。先看 Introduction 中对 KL 误差分解的说明，尤其是 E_init、E_dis、E_SE 的角色，这能帮助把定理放回离散扩散采样流程。然后看问题设定和 score-entropy loss 的形式，确认权重 w_yx、Bregman divergence、density ratio 的定义。核心应放在 minimax lower bound 的定理、对应 estimator 的 upper bound，以及从单时刻 score estimation 推到时间积分 E_SE 的部分。证明细节可以选择性读，但要关注下界构造使用了哪些困难分布，以及上界如何处理小概率状态；这些点最容易转化为对 tokenizer、噪声日程、transition matrix 设计的直觉。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies the finite-sample statistical limit of concrete-score estimation in score-entropy discrete diffusion. Instead of assuming that the learned score is accurate, it asks how well the density ratios p_t(y)/p_t(x) can be estimated from data under the score-entropy loss.","importance":"This is directly relevant to non-autoregressive language modeling based on discrete diffusion. Prior theory for SEDD often decomposes the sampling error into initialization, discretization, and score-estimation terms, then focuses mainly on the first two. This work isolates the third term and characterizes its minimax difficulty. For language modeling, the message is clear: discrete diffusion is constrained not only by sampler design, but also by the statistical hardness of estimating ratios over sparse, long-tailed token distributions.","technical_approach":"$18","experiments":"There are no standard empirical LLM experiments in the provided content. The main results are theoretical: a minimax lower bound for concrete-score estimation and a matching or near-matching upper bound for a simple estimator. The paper also frames these results through the usual SEDD error decomposition: initialization error decays with the diffusion horizon, discretization error depends on the sampler, and the score-estimation error is the finite-sample statistical term. The practical implication is that rare states and large transition neighborhoods make ratio estimation harder, which is especially relevant for text where token distributions are highly imbalanced.","reading_guide":"Read closely in about 1.5–2 hours. Existing sampling-based watermarking methods typically inject position-wise i.i.d. perturbations, which can be poorly aligned with DLM decoding dynamics and degrade generation quality. We propose SAC-Copula, a quality-preserving watermarking method for DLMs based on smooth, locally correlated Gumbel perturbation fields constructed via a Gaussian copu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T08:03:18Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20839.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.CR","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"扩散语言模型（DLM，如 LLaDA）的解码是“并行、多步、可反复修订”的去噪过程，传统面向自回归 LM 的 watermark（尤其是位置独立 i.i.d. 的采样扰动）与这种解码动力学不匹配，容易引入局部不稳定并损伤生成质量。问题是：能否设计一种与 DLM 迭代精炼过程更对齐的 watermark 注入与检测机制，在可检测性与文本质量之间取得更稳的折中？","importance":"对预训练算法本身不是直接贡献，但对“非 AR 建模范式（diffusion LM）在部署侧的可控性/可追溯性”是实用补齐：\n1）把 watermark 设计从“每个位置独立加噪”提升到“设计扰动场的联合结构”，这与 diffusion/denoising 的局部一致性假设更契合；\n2）提出与注入机制匹配的 detector（协方差感知滤波 + native-sample 校准），强调生成侧与检测侧要共同设计；\n3）对 diffusion LM 的训练/采样动态给出机制解释（局部相关降低高频粗糙度），这类分析方式对后续做 DLM decoding/控制信号也有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法属于 sampling-based watermarking，但把“随机扰动的几何结构”作为核心设计维度。\n- 注入端：不再对每个 token 位置注入 i.i.d. Gumbel 扰动，而是构造“平滑、局部自相关”的 Gumbel 扰动场（SAC: Smooth Auto-Correlated）。具体做法是用 Gaussian copula 生成具有指定局部相关结构的联合分布，同时在边缘上保持 Gumbel marginals（在文中假设下）。直觉是：DLM 每步会并行更新多个位置，邻近位置的预测需要在多步中逐渐一致；i.i.d. 的高频扰动会让相邻位置在迭代中互相“打架”，导致振荡或局部不连贯。\n- 机制分析：用“latent perturbation roughness / local-difference energy”刻画扰动场的高频成分，给出从 i.i.d. 到相关场后粗糙度下降的解析表达（文中写到 D_iid=2，D_SAC=2(1-ρ^2 r1) 这类形式），用来支撑“更匹配迭代精炼”的假设。\n- 检测端：提出 SAC-aware detector。核心是协方差感知的滤波（covariance-aware filtering）去读取由相关扰动诱导的证据几何；并用 native-sample calibration 做校准以控制误报。另提供 global-offset scan，用于在 token–signal 轻微错位时做粗同步恢复（更像诊断/稳健性补丁）。\n整体上，它不是改 DLM 训练，而是改 DLM 解码时的 keyed randomness 注入方式，并把检测器做成“匹配滤波器”。","experiments":"实验平台是 LLaDA。作者声称相对“matched 的 i.i.d. Gumbel baseline”，SAC-Copula 在质量–可检测性折中上更好，生成侧最明显的收益是“upper-tail stability”显著改善（可理解为最差情况/尾部风险更小：更少出现局部不稳定导致的糟糕样本）。同时报告了 clean detectability 强、质量维度更均衡，并做了轻度 token-edit stress tests 来诊断 watermark 证据的残留。\n但在给定的全文片段中，关键量化表格与具体数字（例如在固定 FPR 下的 TPR、不同相关长度/ρ 的 ablation、困惑度/人评/自动质量指标、编辑强度下的检测退化曲线）未出现，无法可靠复述具体提升幅度。精读时需要把“检测 ROC/PR 曲线 + 质量指标分布（尤其尾部）”一起看，确认它不是只优化均值而是确实压低了坏样本概率。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（30–45 分钟）：\n1）先看 Figure 1（i.i.d. vs correlated 扰动导致的生成差异示例）和 Figure 2（注入/检测 pipeline），把问题与方案对齐；\n2）精读方法部分里 copula 构造：如何从高斯相关结构得到 Gumbel marginals、相关长度/核如何设定、secret key 如何进入；\n3）看机制分析那段“roughness / local-difference energy”的推导与假设条件：它决定了相关性为何能改善迭代精炼稳定性；\n4）看 detector：covariance-aware filtering 与 calibration 的细节，尤其是如何控制 false positive；\n5）实验优先级：固定误报率下的检出率、质量指标的分位数（tail）、以及对编辑/扰动的稳健性曲线。若只给平均指标而缺少分布/尾部，结论要打折。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Diffusion language models decode via iterative parallel denoising where tokens can be revised across steps. Standard watermarking schemes—especially those injecting position-wise i.i.d. sampling perturbations—are misaligned with this coupled refinement dynamics and can hurt local consistency and overall text quality. The problem is to design a DLM-compatible watermark that preserves quality while remaining reliably detectable.","importance":"Not a pretraining-method contribution, but a practical control/traceability component for non-AR LMs (diffusion LMs) at deployment time. 2","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T09:49:09Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20927.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文处理的是跨模型 Prior methods encode the prompt once with a large frozen mentor and let a smaller frozen student read the resulting latent signal throughout decoding. That static signal can become stale as the generated prefix grows. In long outputs, the student’s next decision depends on the prompt plus its own generated history, while the mentor memory still describes only the initial prompt.","importance":"The main value is the clean separation between capability limits and guidance freshness. The work shows that latent transfer from a large model to a small model is not simply a one-shot prefill problem when generation is long. The interface must track the evolving prefix. This matters for model compression and serving: a large model can provide low-frequency latent updates, while a small model performs most decoding steps. The contribution is mainly a diagnostic and serving-oriented method rather than a new pretraining objective.","technical_approach":"The paper first uses a training-free intervention called OracleSwap. Every fixed number of generated tokens, the mentor re-prefills the prompt plus current generated prefix, rebuilds the memory, and swaps it in. The student weights, generated text, and student KV cache are left unchanged, so the intervention isolates the effect of memory freshness. MentorPulse then makes this practical. Mentor states are compressed into a capped, position-aware slot memory. The student reads this memory through a gated cross-attention bridge. Refreshing is versioned and incremental: the mentor reuses its KV cache and processes only newly generated tokens, then replaces the memory without invalidating the student’s decoding state. Windowed Refresh Training trains the bridge on output windows associated with sampled memory versions. The refresh interval R sets the quality-cost trade-off; one-shot guidance is recovered as R goes to infinity.","experiments":"$1a","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 25–35 minutes. We assign each token hidden carrier \\(h_i\\) an active-presence coefficient \\(p_i=\\lVert h_i\\rVert^2/(τ+\\lVert h_i\\rVert^2)\\). oth","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T14:43:45Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21174.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作讨论 attention 模型里的零向量 token 是否能成为真正“不参与计算”的状态。标准 attention mask 控制的是 query-source 关系，但零向量本身并不会自然惰性化：零 query 会得到可见 value 的平均，零 key/value source 仍会向 softmax denominator 加入 exp(0)。","importance":"对 LLM 预训练的启发是架构边界条件。padding、空 latent slot、稀疏路由槽、缺失模态 token、可变长度 batch 都需要一种状态级 NULL，而不是只依赖关系级 mask。工作给出一个比较干净的算子约束：零向量 token 在 attention、norm、FFN、注入和统计操作中都不应影响已有 token。它还没有证明大规模 LLM 训练收益，因此更适合泛读，作为设计 zero-vector token can be made truly inert in an attention model. Standard masks control allowed query-source relations, but they do not make the zero vector a non-participating state. A zero query still returns an average over visible values, and a zero key-value source still contributes exp(0) to the softmax denominator.","importance":"The relevance to foundation-model research is mostly architectural. Padding, empty latent slots, sparse routing positions, missing modality tokens, and variable-support computation all need a state-level NULL in addition to relation-level masks. The work gives a precise operator contract for this NULL behavior: a zero carrier should emit no update and should add no support mass to shared computations. It is not yet evidence for improved large-scale LLM pretraining, but it is a useful design reference for masked, latent, sparse, or multimodal models.","technical_approach":"Each hidden carrier h_i is assigned a smooth presence coefficient p_i = ||h_i||^2 / (tau + ||h_i||^2). The same coefficient is used in two places. As a receiver gate, p_i multiplies the token’s output. As a source mass, p_j weights that source in both the attention numerator and the partition function. OAttention keeps standard dot-product scoring, external visibility masks, exponential competition, and value aggregation, but separates token presence from relation visibility. The broader O-Closure principle extends the same idea beyond attention. Token-local maps must absorb zero, while support-coupled maps must assign zero mass to null sources. OFFN, ONorm, OInject, and OStandardize cover feed-forward branches, normalization, injected signals such as position information, and shared statistics. Residual addition and composition then give a conditional construction of an OTransformer.","experiments":"The empirical evidence is scoped. First, finite-precision tests verify the declared null-consistency properties: a null receiver returns zero, inserted null sources do not change previous outputs or weights, a zero carrier inserted into self-attention leaves old tokens unchanged and gives the new token zero output, and empty support returns finite zero outputs. Second, a frozen pretrained TabPFN v3 host is used to test calibrated near-identity compatibility, showing that the modified operators can be inserted under controlled conditions without immediately breaking the host. Third, a scoped OTransformer ablation shows that changing attention alone is insufficient; the whole computation path needs O-Closure. There are no large-scale LLM pretraining results, and the guarantees do not cover arbitrary tokenizers, routers, KV caches, or readouts.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough for most LLM researchers. relevance is inference and agent-context design rather than pretraining itself. The useful signal is that structured tool schemas need not always be encoded as ordinary dense text. If cross-resource interactions are weak, each schema can be represented as a composable KV block. This has implications for tool-aware context layouts, resource tokenization, prefill caching, and possibly training-time attention constraints for agentic models.","technical_approach":"The framework has three components. First, resource-wise attention removes attention across different resource blocks and resets positions within each block. This makes the KV state of a resource independent of neighboring resources and global order, enabling independent construction and reuse. Because this differs from full-context prefill, the model is lightly fine-tuned to adapt to the new attention and positional pattern. Second, structural pruning keeps resource visibility only on selected layer and KV-head-group routes. The routes are ranked by marginal contribution to prediction loss, rather than by attention mass, which is a better proxy for output utility. Third, semantic pruning keeps invocation-critical fields such as resource identifiers, argument names, and constraints, together with a suffix token that acts as a compact semantic anchor.","experiments":"The evaluation benchmark is built from seven public tool- and skill-use datasets. The authors use diversity-first sampling to increase resource coverage, filter invalid traces with hallucinated resource calls, and create both in-distribution and resource-disjoint out-of-distribution splits. On Qwen3 backbones, resource-wise attention stays within 0.2% of the dense-attention baseline on effectiveness metrics while improving time to first token by 3.655×. With structural and semantic pruning, allocated KV-tensor memory drops by 92.43% and attention becomes 1.423× faster. Under matched structural budgets, contribution-based route selection preserves invocation quality better than attention-based and layer-asymmetric alternatives, especially on unseen resources. Field-aware semantic pruning also outperforms generic text-compression baselines in the tested settings. Exhaustive profiling resolves this uncertainty, but measures many configurations that do not affect the final resource allocation. We present FleetSieve, which selects measurements according to their expected effect on a resource-coupled, SLO-aware fleet decision. FleetSieve models capacity and tail latency jointly","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-20T05:54:29Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_19659.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.DC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 LLM serving fleet 配置中的一个决策问题：在固定 GPU 预算下，面对不同 workload class、不同 SLO、不同请求长度和负载水平，如何选择每类业务的 tensor parallel degree 与 replica 数量。难点在于 TP 对吞吐、尾延迟、显存余量和副本数的影响并不单调，而且最优选择依赖全局资源耦合。传统做法是把所有候选配置都 profile 一遍，但很多测量其实不会改变最终 fleet allocation。作者要做的不是更准确地拟合性能曲线，而是更少地测量、但仍足以确定最终资源分配决策。","importance":"它对基座预训练本身不是核心论文，但对大模型系统研究有一个明确启发：很多 profiling 问题不该以“把性能表补全”为目标，而该以“是否会改变下游决策”为目标。这个思路对训练系统也有可迁移性，例如并行策略选择、混合精度配置、checkpoint/activation trade-off、不同 batch/sequence shape 的 recipe 搜索，都可能从 decision-aware profiling 中受益。就当前论文本身而言，贡献更偏 serving 侧的资源配置方法，而不是训练或推理算法创新，因此相关性有限，适合泛读。","technical_approach":"FleetSieve 的核心做法是把 profiling 视为 downstream decision identification。系统对每个 workload-class × 配置单元维护 capacity 区间和 tail-latency 区间，而不是单点估计。然后在这些不确定区间上分别求两个 allocation：一个保守解，用 lower capacity + upper tail；一个乐观解，用 upper capacity + lower tail。两者共享同一个 fleet 约束和分层目标：先满足关键业务的最低 fulfillment floor，再最大化最小满足率，再最大化总 goodput，最后减少碎片化。\n\n算法的关键不在于复杂预测模型，而在 acquisition rule。它优先测量那些最可能缩小“保守解与乐观解之间决策差距”的配置，而不是优先覆盖网格、也不是优先减少局部预测不确定性。换句话说，某个 cell 即便预测方差很大，只要无论怎么变都不会改变最终 allocation，它就不值得先测。相反，一个看似普通的 cell，如果可能让某个 workload 从 SLO infeasible 变成 feasible，或者改变关键类业务的资源占用，就会被优先选择。\n\n论文还给了一个条件式 decision certificate。状态分三类：Certified-Feasible、Undecided、Certified-Infeasible。这里的“certified”不是分布无关的严格保证，而是建立在经验不确定性模型覆盖真实性能表的前提上。作者对此表述比较克制，明确说这是 empirical / conditionally calibrated 的证书，而不是绝对正确性声明。这一点写得比较严谨。","experiments":"实验设置相对集中：单个 31B 开源 decoder 模型，单节点 H100，FP8，基于 vLLM，关闭 speculative decoding；候选 TP 为 {2,4,8}。每个 profiling cell 跑 300 秒，目标是比较 FleetSieve 与均匀栅格测量在达到 oracle aggregate decision 时需要多少 GPU-seconds。\n\n主结果是：在固定 H100 measurement grid 上，FleetSieve 用 22,200 GPU-seconds 达到 oracle aggregate decision，比 uniform profiling 少 6.9%。作者没有把结果包装得过头，反而明确指出收益是选择性的：对 Chat workload 节省更明显，对 Code workload 则没有节省。这说明方法成立的前提是“仍有未测量单元可能改变全局 allocation”；如果某类 workload 的最优配置很快就被锁定，decision-aware acquisition 的额外建模未必带来收益。正文里还有对 capacity 与 tail jointly modeled 的必要性说明：只看吞吐会把高 throughput 但尾延迟不满足 SLO 的配置误判为可行，这会直接污染 fleet 级决策。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Problem Formulation 和 FleetSieve 方法部分，理解它的目标不是性能预测，而是 allocation gap 缩减。然后看实验里的两个对比：一是与 uniform profiling 的 GPU-seconds 差异，二是 Chat 与 Code 两类 workload 为什么收益不同。若只花 20 分钟，重点看 Figure/表格里 conservative vs optimistic allocation gap 的收敛过程，以及 certificate 的停止条件。\n\n阅读时要带着一个边界判断：这不是通用 BO 或 active learning 新理论，而是一个很工程化的 decision-aware profiler。若关心训练系统，可思考它的思想如何迁移到并行策略搜索或训练 recipe profiling；若只关心 LLM 预训练本身，这篇的优先级不高。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a serving-fleet decision problem: under a fixed GPU budget, multiple workload classes, different SLOs, and different request shapes, how should one choose tensor-parallel degree and replica count for each class? The difficulty is that TP affects throughput, tail latency, memory headroom, and replica multiplicity in a non-monotonic way, and the best choice depends on global resource coupling. Exhaustive profiling resolves this by measuring every candidate configuration, but many measurements are irrelevant because they cannot change the final fleet allocation. The paper aims to reduce profiling while still identifying the same downstream allocation decision.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it carries one useful systems lesson: profiling should often be optimized for decision impact, not for table completion or local predictive accuracy. That idea can transfer to training systems as well, for example when choosing parallelism strategies, mixed-precision settings, activation-checkpoint trade-offs, or recipe configurations under hardware constraints. For this specific work, the contribution is mainly on the serving side, in SLO-aware fleet configuration, rather than on training or inference algorithms themselves. So its relevance to foundation-model pretraining is limited, and it fits a skim read.","technical_approach":"$1f","experiments":"$20","reading_guide":"For a skim read, start with the problem formulation and the FleetSieve method section. The main conceptual shift is that the target is not better performance prediction, but faster reduction of allocation uncertainty. Then inspect the experimental comparison against uniform profiling, especially the difference between Chat and Code workloads. If you only have 20 minutes, focus on the figures or tables showing how the conservative-versus-optimistic allocation gap shrinks over time, and on the stopping conditions behind the certificate.\n\nIt is useful to read this with the right boundary in mind. This is not a new general theory of Bayesian optimization or active learning. It is a practical decision-aware profiler for serving fleets. If your interest is training systems, the transferable part is the decision-aware profiling principle. If your interest is strictly LLM pretraining, this paper is lower priority.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"解决 LLM Serving 集群配置搜索的效率问题，对负责推理部署的工程师有参考价值，预训练研究员可略读其建模思路。","en":"Addresses the efficiency of configuration search for LLM serving fleets, valuable for deployment engineers; pretraining researchers can skim its modeling approach."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Huang Cheng","Scott Zhang","Aubert Li"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["LLM Serving","Performance Profiling","Tensor Parallelism","SLO"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19659","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.19659","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.19677","title":"CacheRoute: Planned Prefix-Affinity Routing for Large-Scale LLM Serving","abstract":"Prefix caching avoids prefill only when a repeated request returns to a server that still holds the prefix KV. Cache-blind balancing disperses that reuse; fixed affinity preserves it but can overload a server. CacheRoute resolves this tradeoff with a periodic routing plan. It admits high-rate keys to a stable warm set and places their assignments by expected load. Hot keys may use more than one destination, although every key in our primary semi-synthetic aggregate uses exactly one. On Llama-3.3","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-20T06:12:47Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_19677.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在大规模 LLM serving 中，prefix KV-cache 只有在“相同前缀的请求回到仍持有该前缀的同一目的地”时才省掉 prefill。常规的 cache-blind 负载均衡会把同一前缀打散到多台机器，导致回访间隔变长、前缀变冷；而固定 affinity（例如一致性哈希）虽然保住局部性，却把 key 的流量偏斜直接映射成单机排队，p99 被最热目的地拖垮。论文要解决的是：在多租户、强 key-skew、且每个 key 有可复用上下文的真实工作负载下，如何同时获得高 KV 命中率与可控的尾延迟/容量。","importance":"这不是预训练方法论文，但对“训练-推理闭环”里的推理侧成本结构很关键：它把 prefix 复用从“缓存实现细节”提升为“路由与容量规划问题”，并给出可操作的规划式（planned）路由策略与大规模硬件实测边界。对做大模型训练的人，直接启发在于：\n1) 真实线上 prompt 的可复用部分往往只占一小段（文中约 180 tokens、约 15%），但在高 QPS 下仍能显著改变吞吐与 SLO；这会反过来影响你在 pretrain/posttrain 里对上下文模板、系统提示、检索块稳定性的设计取舍。\n2) 评估 serving 策略不能只看 hit rate，还要看“hit rate 提升是否抵消了负载不均带来的排队尾部”；文中给出了可复现的测量方法（knee 点、paired runs、burst 测试、shadow replay）。\n整体贡献量级偏工程系统，但实验规模（70B、60 H100）和对 tradeoff 的刻画比较扎实。","technical_approach":"CacheRoute 的核心是“周期性规划的 prefix-affinity 路由表”，用稳定性换取缓存驻留与可预测负载：\n- 观测与建模对象：按业务 key（多租户场景中的 business）统计到达率 λ_b，并把一个 serving fleet 抽象为 R 个可路由目的地（destination；70B 实验里一个 destination 是 TP=2 的一组）。\n- 两阶段决策：先决定哪些 key 进入 warm set（被显式做 affinity），再决定每个 key 分配到哪些目的地、以及这些分配如何在全局上摆放。\n- Admission（top-rate admission）：在有限 warm-prefix slot 预算下，优先接纳高到达率 key，使其前缀更可能在 eviction 前回访命中。未接纳流量回退到 cache-blind 的 power-of-two choices。\n- Load control（按 knee 点设 q_cap）：从单个目的地的 latency/load knee 校准一个容量阈值 q_cap。对每个 key，给它分配足够多的目的地副本数，使得该 key 分摊到每个目的地的期望负载不超过 q_cap。这里的“复制”是为削峰，不是为提高命中率本身。\n- Placement（LPT 列表调度）：把（key, 副本数）形成的负载块用 longest-processing-time-first 做列表调度，尽量均衡各目的地的期望负载，从而避免纯 affinity 的最热桶效应。\n- 控制平面：路由表在一个 control interval 内固定不变，避免每请求的反应式重映射导致的‘刚热就被挪走’。\n- 论文也明确边界：top-rate admission 与 LPT 都不是新算法点，创新在于把“缓存局部性 + 负载上界 + 稳定路由表”组合成可落地的路由设计，并用大规模测量证明在哪些工作负载区间值得开启、哪些区间应保持普通负载均衡。","experiments":"主实验：Llama-3.3-70B，fp8，60×H100，30 个 TP2 destinations。\n- 在 p99 ≤ 3.5s 的 SLO 下，CacheRoute 可持续吞吐 1176±11 QPS；最强 baseline（Preble）为 511±11 QPS，约 2.3×。\n- 在 offered load=100 QPS 时，CacheRoute p99=1.8s；五个 baseline 的 p99 为 3.8–8.5s。\n- KV-cache hit rate：cache-blind balancing 为 64.1±1.3%，CacheRoute 为 93.2±0.5%。\n- 第二个半合成 aggregate：在更宽 active set 下优势约 1.6×。\n机制/消融（8B 控制实验，用于制造更极端热 key 以测试复制）：\n- affinity 提升 hit rate；LPT placement 把负载不均从 3.46× 降到 1.24×，并把 SLO knee 从 240 QPS 推到 ≥500 QPS。\n工作负载刻画（重要，因为解释了为什么“只做缓存”不够）：\n- 128,824 个业务 key，Gini=0.756；约 4% key 贡献 47% 请求；单个 key 最大占比 <0.3%。\n- 平均输入 1.2K tokens，其中约 90% 在当前 turn 之前；可路由带来复用的 key-specific 段约 180 tokens（约 15%）。\n- few-shot 检索块变化导致 67% 的连续请求对不共享精确前缀，进一步强调“可复用前缀很短但很关键”。","reading_guide":"建议按“失败模式 → 规划器 → 大规模实测 → 机制解释”的顺序读：\n1) Workload and Failure Mode：看清楚为什么 fleet 变大反而让 prefix 变冷（回访间隔 ~ R/λ_b）以及为什么纯 affinity 会把 skew 变成尾延迟。\n2) CacheRoute 设计细节：重点抓三个旋钮——top-rate admission、q_cap 的校准方式（knee 点）、LPT placement；理解“复制用于限载而非命中率”的分工。\n3) 70B/60 H100 的 paired runs 与 SLO knee 图：这是论文最有说服力的部分，注意它如何同时报告 hit rate、吞吐、p99，并展示 tie/overload 区间。\n4) 8B ablation：用来验证机制（affinity vs placement vs replication），尤其是 3.46×→1.24× 的不均衡改善如何对应到 knee 的右移。\n预计泛读 30–45 分钟能抓住主线；想复现/落地需要更细看控制周期、warm set 预算与 shadow replay 的测量方法。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Prefix KV caching only saves prefill when repeated requests land on a destination that still retains the prefix KV. Cache-blind load balancing disperses repeated prefixes across the fleet, increasing revisit time and causing eviction; strict affinity (e.g., consistent hashing) restores locality but turns key popularity skew into queue skew, letting the hottest destination dominate p99 latency. The paper targets a practical question in multi-tenant, skewed, prefix-reusing workloads: how to jointly maximize KV hit rate and keep tail latency/capacity under control at large scale.","importance":"This is a serving-systems paper rather than pretraining, but it matters for the cost envelope that pretraining teams ultimately optimize against. It reframes prefix reuse as a routing/planning problem and provides a measured operating regime on a large setup (70B on 60 H100s). Two concrete takeaways for foundation-model researchers: (1) the reusable portion of real prompts can be small (≈180 tokens, ~15% here) yet still materially shift throughput and SLO, which feeds back into how stable you keep system templates / retrieved blocks; (2) evaluating caching strategies requires coupling hit rate with queueing imbalance—higher hit rate can still reduce capacity if it increases skew. The paper’s value is in the design that enforces stability plus load caps, and in the scale/rigor of the measurements (paired runs, knee-point calibration, burst tests, shadow replay).","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"Main hardware evaluation: Llama-3.3-70B, fp8, 60×H100, 30 TP2 destinations.\n- Sustained throughput under p99 ≤ 3.5s: 1176±11 QPS for CacheRoute vs 511±11 QPS for the strongest baseline (Preble), ~2.3×.\n- At 100 offered QPS: p99 is 1.8s for CacheRoute vs 3.8–8.5s across five baselines.\n- Served KV-cache hit rate: 64.1±1.3% (cache-blind) → 93.2±0.5% (CacheRoute).\n- Second semi-synthetic aggregate: ~1.6× advantage with a broader active set.\nMechanism/ablation on an 8B setup (to force extremely hot keys and exercise replication): affinity increases hit rate; 参数。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"HYDRA targets design-space exploration for serving hybrid Transformer–Mamba LLMs on heterogeneous chiplet systems. Static workload mapping is not accurate enough for this setting.","importance":"The paper is not a direct pretraining contribution, but it is useful for judging the deployability of hybrid LLM architectures. For Transformer–SSM models, training loss and long-context quality are only part of the story. Serving behavior, memory bandwidth, inter-chiplet communication, and runtime scheduling can change whether the architecture is attractive in practice. HYDRA gives a concrete way to reason about these constraints.","technical_approach":"HYDRA jointly explores architecture and runtime policy. The search space covers chiplet composition, placement, inter-chiplet bandwidth, dynamic batching, and task scheduling. It models the different behavior of Transformer and Mamba blocks, and separates prefill from decode: prefill is more compute-intensive and parallel, while decode makes Transformer layers increasingly sensitive to KV-cache traffic, whereas Mamba layers keep a relatively stable recurrent-state footprint. The framework uses communication-aware placement and elastic scheduling for multi-tenant serving. To avoid exhaustive simulation, it introduces a Markov-based performance estimator that models transitions among chiplet states and quickly ranks candidate designs. Detailed simulation is then used on selected configurations to capture NoI effects, transient contention, memory management, batching decisions, and pipeline imbalance.","experiments":"Across the evaluated workloads, HYDRA reports 1.55× average throughput improvement and 43.7% lower TTFT. Standard speculative decoding usually searches for a cheap token-level draft distribution. SSR instead uses the temporal structure of the reasoning trace: a partial CoT already induces an intermediate answer distribution, and that distribution tends to move closer to the final answer as reasoning progresses. This is also interesting for model researchers because it treats CoT as a gradually converging distributional process rather than only a human-readable rationale. The method is training-free and requires no auxiliary heads or architectural changes.","technical_approach":"SSR runs the same model at two effective reasoning budgets. A partial CoT produces draft answer tokens, while the completed CoT serves as the verifier. Draft generation happens concurrently with continued CoT generation, so the draft cost can be hidden behind ongoing reasoning. Verification first accepts the longest left-to-right prefix that matches the final answer. For long-form outputs, early differences in phrasing or variable names can cause prefix verification to fail even when later spans still overlap. To recover this, SSR adds suffix decoding: it builds a suffix cache from the draft and proposes later matching spans after the first rejection. The paper also introduces an iterative variant that samples drafts at multiple points during CoT generation; earlier drafts are reused to bootstrap later drafts rather than being wasted.","experiments":"The provided content describes the method and claims but does not include the full experimental tables. distributions for multiple future positions in one pass. Existing DLM tree builders such as DDTree assemble trees from per-position high-probability tokens using marginal token probabilities only. That misses parent-child compatibility: a token that the target would accept may already be present in the tree, but attached to the wrong parent path. The second issue is fixed node budget. A diffusion-style block head such as DFlash is an attractive drafter, predicting an entire block of future tokens in one forward pass. However, it is trained on per-position marginals rather than the joint block distribution, so the tokens it emits are individually plausible yet jointly incoherent. Together these steps degrade reasoning, mathematics, coding, and long-context behavior enough to require a recovery, or healing, stage before deployment. The default recipe, quantization-aware training (QAT), re-fits the compressed, quantized model to hard labels; in our pipeline it converged slowly and collapsed past its peak. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T10:19:27Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20953.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG","cs.PF"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是结构化压缩之后再做 4-bit 量化时的恢复问题。常见流程会先把大模型通过删头、删 FFN 神经元、删层或降维等方式压到更小架构，再量化到 4 bit。两步叠加会损伤数学、推理、代码和长上下文能力。直接做 QAT 收敛慢且容易过峰后崩掉；用量化感知蒸馏 QAD 时，如果 teacher 选压缩后的 bf16 checkpoint，又会被这个已经退化的中间模型限制住。","importance":"这篇论文对基座研究的价值主要在部署前恢复和压缩训练 recipe，而不是预训练算法。关键启发是：在 compound compression 场景里，量化不是一个单独的后处理步骤，它可以作为第二次接受强 teacher 监督的阶段。teacher 选择比 fake quant 技巧本身更重要。对于需要发布小参数、低 bit 模型的团队，这个结论会影响压缩链路设计：不要默认把压缩后 bf16 模型当作量化 teacher，原始未压缩模型的 logits 可能提供更高上限。","technical_approach":"Quantization-Aware Healing 使用已经完成 post-training 的原始未压缩模型作为 teacher，直接蒸馏结构化压缩且 4-bit fake-quantized 的 student。流程里，原始高精度模型 M_HP 先经过结构化压缩算子 S 得到更小架构 M_C；M_C 再通过 KL 蒸馏从 M_HP 恢复为 bf16 checkpoint M_BF16；随后量化算子 Q 把 M_BF16 转成 MXFP4。标准 QAD 会用 M_BF16 监督 4-bit student，但 M_BF16 本身只是压缩后恢复模型，并非独立训练出来的强 teacher。QAH 改为用 M_HP 的输出分布监督 4-bit student。由于 KL 蒸馏只依赖输出分布，teacher 和 student 不需要同构。这个改动简单，但直接避开了“用弱中间 checkpoint 固定学生上限”的问题。","experiments":"实验链路是 GPT-OSS 120B 压缩到 60B，再量化为 MXFP4。论文报告 QAH student 在 9 个 benchmark 中有 7 个达到或超过自己的 bf16 source，并且在 LiveCodeBench 上达到原始 120B 模型水平。资源上，student 参数量是 teacher 的一半，权重内存约低 4 倍。与匹配的 QAT baseline 相比，QAH 收敛约快 7 倍，并且不依赖手工调 early stopping；QAT 在过峰后会出现 collapse，部署上更难控。论文还记录了不同分布式训练 backend 之间存在较大且可复现的质量差异，这是实际复现和上线时需要注意的工程变量。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20 到 30 分钟。先看 introduction 里对 QAT、QAD、QAH 三者适用场景的区分，重点是为什么结构化压缩之后不存在一个理想的同架构 full-precision teacher。然后看方法部分的三阶段模型定义：M_HP、M_BF16、MXFP4 student，以及 KL 蒸馏目标如何跨架构工作。实验部分重点看 120B→60B→MXFP4 的 benchmark 表、QAT 训练曲线、QAH 收敛速度和 early stopping 稳定性。第 6 节的部署经验值得快速读，尤其是分布式 backend 造成质量差异这一点。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses recovery after compound compression: structural compression followed by 4-bit quantization. Structural compression changes the architecture and reduces parameter count; quantization then lowers precision. Together they hurt reasoning, math, coding, and long-context behavior. Standard QAT is slow and fragile in this setting. QAD is safer for quantization-only pipelines, but after structural compression its natural teacher is the recovered bf16 compressed model, which is already a degraded approximation of the original model.","importance":"The useful lesson is about the compression and deployment recipe. In compound compression, the quantization stage should not be treated only as a lossy conversion step. It can be another distillation stage, and the choice of teacher matters more than the presence of fake quantization alone. For teams building smaller low-bit variants of large models, the paper argues against using the compressed bf16 checkpoint as the default teacher for quantization recovery. The original uncompressed model can provide a higher-quality training signal even when teacher and student architectures differ.","technical_approach":"Quantization-Aware Healing trains the compressed 4-bit student with fake quantization while distilling directly from the original uncompressed model. The pipeline starts with a high-precision model M_HP that has already gone through post-training. A structural operator S produces a smaller architecture M_C. This compressed model is recovered in bf16 by KL distillation from M_HP, giving M_BF16. A quantization operator then produces an MXFP4 student. Standard QAD would use M_BF16 as the teacher, but that teacher has already absorbed compression loss. QAH instead uses the output distribution of M_HP as the target for the 4-bit student. Because the loss is distribution-level KL, the teacher and student do not need to share an architecture.","experiments":"The reported pipeline compresses GPT-OSS 120B to 60B and then quantizes it to MXFP4. The QAH student matches or exceeds its bf16 source on 7 of 9 benchmarks and reaches the original 120B model on LiveCodeBench. It uses half the teacher’s parameters and roughly 4x less weight memory. Compared with a matched QAT baseline, QAH converges about 7x faster and is less dependent on manually tuned early stopping. The paper also reports a large and reproducible quality gap between distributed training backends, which is an important operational detail for reproducing the recipe.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 20–30 minutes. We frame calibration-data selection for quantization as a target-dependent uncertainty-preservation problem. Different deployments emphasize different regions of the input distribution, yet prior work mainly optimizes accuracy-oriented compression metrics or adjusts scores after quantization. We formalize this ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T12:07:49Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21019.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是量化后精度掉多少，而是量化后模型的“不确定性行为”还能不能保持和全精度模型一致。更具体地说，作者把 calibration data selection 重新定义为一个目标相关的问题：如果部署场景关心的是整体概率分布、置信度、top-2 margin、重排序分数，那么应优化 distributional preservation；如果关心的是 answerability、abstention、低 margin 决策边界是否翻转，那么应优化 boundary preservation。论文要解决的核心问题，是在不改量化器本身的前提下，如何选择一小批校准数据，让量化模型尽量保留全精度模型在这些不确定性信号上的行为，而不是只保留 top-1 正确率。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在预训练本身，而在“模型压缩后哪些行为会被破坏、哪些信号值得保真”这件事上给出了更细的视角。很多训练和后训练工作默认 logits、confidence、margin 这些量在部署压缩后仍可用，但这篇文章说明：即便 top-1 不变，置信度结构也可能明显漂移，进而影响 abstention、reranking、verification、ensemble 等依赖分数的系统。它的贡献量级更像一个部署侧方法学补丁，而不是范式变化：一是把 uncertainty preservation 明确成量化校准目标；二是给出 target-dependent 的形式化风险；三是用 mixture-mismatch 论证说明不存在对所有目标都合适的单一 calibration recipe。对做 pretrain/post-train 接口的人，这个结论有现实意义：训练时学到的概率结构，未必能在低比特部署中自然保留下来。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者提出 DPQ（Doubt-Preserving Quantization），它不是新的量化算法，而是一族 pre-quantization calibration data recipe。核心做法是先用全精度模型在候选样本上打分，再按部署目标挑选“脆弱样本”。两类信号最关键：一类是 high-doubt 样本，比如高熵、低置信度、小 top-2 margin，这些样本对分布扰动更敏感；另一类是 answerability boundary 附近的样本，尤其适合 SQuAD2 这类 answer/no-answer 决策。然后把这些 target-aware 样本与 generic anchors 混合，送入原封不动的量化器，例如 GPTQ、AWQ 等。论文的理论部分不复杂，但有一个有用的判断：如果目标分布更重视边界附近样本，而 calibration mixture 主要由普通 anchor 组成，那么最优校准集不会与面向 broad MCQA 的最优校准集一致；反过来也一样。这就是作者所谓 mixture-mismatch。实验里 DPQ 有多种 recipe，例如按 boundary signal 和 generic anchor 的比例配比，像 s128-r75 这类配置对应固定样本数和混合比。文章还区分了两类 preservation risk：一类是分布层面的风险，关注量化后概率分布与 FP 分布的偏离；另一类是边界层面的风险，关注低 margin 区域的决策翻转，并可用权重函数强调 answerability 或 selective prediction 场景。这个 framing 比单纯看 ECE 或 accuracy 更贴近实际系统。","experiments":"实验覆盖 8 个语言模型、9 个 NLP benchmark、22 种比较方法或配置，评测维度明显比一般量化论文更偏“行为保真”。指标除了常见的 ECE、adaptive ECE、NLL、Brier score，还包括和全精度行为直接对齐的 FP agreement、JSD(pQ, p0)、confidence shift、top-two margin drift、margin correlation，以及 SQuAD2 上的 answerability boundary 指标。主要结论有三点。第一，最优 calibration recipe 明显依赖目标：SQuAD2 这类边界敏感任务上，高 boundary mixture 的 DPQ-s128-r75 表现最好；而 broad MCQA 更偏好较温和的 recipe，如 DPQ-r50、只看 confidence 或只看 entropy 的选择策略。第二，单看 accuracy 往往掩盖了不确定性漂移；有些配置 top-1 基本不变，但 confidence/margin 指标明显恶化。第三，信号有一定可迁移性，但量化器相关性仍然存在：作者做了 post-hoc calibration 和 AWQ 分析，发现 preservation signal 不是完全绑定某一个量化器，但 score-based metric 的最优 recipe 仍带有 quantizer-specific 特征。摘要里没有给出完整逐表数字，全文重点也不是追求某个统一平均提升，而是证明“目标依赖”这个现象在多模型多任务上稳定存在。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不需要逐式推导。建议先看 Introduction 和 Problem Statement，抓住作者为什么把 calibration data selection 从 accuracy 导向改成 uncertainty-preservation 导向。然后重点看定义的两类风险：distributional risk 和 boundary risk，它们对应两种部署目标。接着看 mixture-mismatch 的论证，不必纠结形式证明，关键是理解为什么不存在一个对所有任务都稳妥的固定 calibration mixture。实验部分优先看 SQuAD2 与 MCQA 的对比表，以及各类 FP-behavior 指标，那里最能体现“accuracy 不变但 uncertainty 变了”。如果时间有限，30 到 40 分钟足够抓住主线。值得深挖的点有两个：一是这种 target-aware calibration 是否能推广到更大模型和更极端低比特；二是能否把 preservation target 直接并入量化目标，而不是只在数据选择层面做文章。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not about preserving top-1 accuracy under quantization. It asks a narrower and more deployment-relevant question: can a quantized LM preserve the uncertainty behavior of its full-precision counterpart? The authors cast calibration-data selection as a target-dependent uncertainty-preservation problem. If the downstream use cares about probability distributions, confidence, margins, reranking, or ensembling, the objective is distributional preservation. If it cares about abstention, answerability, or low-margin decision boundaries, the objective is boundary preservation. The core problem is how to choose a small calibration set, without changing the quantizer itself, so that the quantized model stays behaviorally close to the FP model in these uncertainty-sensitive regimes.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model researchers is indirect but real. Many training and post-training pipelines assume that logits, confidence, and margins remain usable after low-bit deployment. This paper shows that this assumption is weak: the top prediction can stay unchanged while confidence structure shifts enough to affect abstention, verification, reranking, and selective prediction. The contribution is methodological rather than paradigm-level. It formalizes uncertainty preservation as a first-class quantization objective, separates target distributions into distributional vs boundary-sensitive cases, and argues that no single calibration recipe should be expected to work across all targets. For people working on the pretrain-to-deployment interface, the message is simple: probability structure learned during training is not automatically preserved by compression.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Start with the introduction and problem formulation to understand why the authors move from accuracy-oriented calibration to uncertainty-preserving calibration. Focus on the two preservation risks, because they map cleanly to two deployment regimes. Then read the mixture-mismatch argument; the exact proof matters less than the practical implication that one fixed calibration mixture is not universally appropriate. In the experiments, prioritize the SQuAD2 vs MCQA comparisons and the FP-behavior metrics. That is where the paper makes its case most clearly: unchanged accuracy does not imply preserved uncertainty. A 30-40 minute read is enough to extract the main idea. If you want to go deeper, the natural follow-up questions are whether this extends to larger models and lower-bit settings, and whether uncertainty preservation can be built into the quantization objective itself rather than handled only through calibration-data selection.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"量化论文里少见地把 uncertainty 当主目标，不再只看 perplexity/accuracy；先读 mixture-mismatch 论证和 calibration set 构造，若指标定义站得住，这会影响你做拒答与 verifier 的部署 recipe。","en":"Few quantization papers treat uncertainty as the primary objective instead of just perplexity or accuracy. This makes ordinary decoding memory-efficient, but hurts speculative decoding. To verify a batch of draft tokens and then roll back the rejected ones, today's systems snapshot the full recurrent state at every draft position for GDN layers, and those snapshots cannot be shared across branches of a draft tree, so a wid","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T10:31:15Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20961.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.DC","cs.LG","cs.PF"],"summary":{"core_problem":"TreeWY 解决的是混合架构模型在 speculative decoding 中的 Gated DeltaNet 回滚问题。GDN 层不像 softmax attention 那样保留逐 token KV cache，而是把前缀压到固定大小的 recurrent state 里。普通解码时这很省内存，但验证 draft token 或 draft tree 时，系统需要回滚到被接受节点的状态。现有做法通常为每个 draft 位置保存完整 recurrent state，树状 draft 又无法共享这些快照，导致验证阶段的瞬时显存随 draft 节点数快速增长。","importance":"这篇对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对混合 attention/linear-attention 架构的落地很关键。GDN、Mamba 类层被引入大模型，一个主要动机是降低长上下文 KV cache 成本；如果 speculative decoding 反而要求大量 state snapshot，服务端吞吐优势会被抵消。TreeWY 说明这类 recurrent linear-attention 层需要配套的验证算法，而不能直接套用 Transformer 的 KV rollback 机制。对基座研究的启发是：架构设计不能只看训练 loss 和单步解码内存，也要看 speculative verification、tree decoding、rollback 等 serving 路径是否有代数结构可用。","technical_approach":"核心方法是把 Gated DeltaNet 的 gated delta recurrence 改写成树结构上的 WY/UT 形式。GDN 的状态转移是标量 decay 乘以 rank-1 correction：T_t = alpha_t(I - GDN layers keep a fixed-size recurrent state instead of a token-growing KV cache. That is attractive for normal decoding, but verification of draft chains or draft trees requires rollback to the accepted node. Current systems usually snapshot the full recurrent state at every draft position, and those snapshots cannot be shared across branches, making wide speculative trees memory-expensive.","importance":"The work is not a pretraining method, but it matters for the practical viability of hybrid attention and linear-attention LMs. GDN-style layers are often motivated by lower KV-cache growth at long context. If speculative decoding requires full recurrent-state snapshots, the serving advantage can disappear during verification. The useful lesson for base-model research is that architecture choices should be evaluated together with the decoding algorithms they enable or block. A recurrent layer needs algebraic structure not only for training and single-token decoding, but also for rollback, tree verification, and commit.","technical_approach":"The method rewrites the Gated DeltaNet recurrence into a tree-structured WY/UT form. A GDN transition is a scalar decay times a rank-1 correction, T_t = alpha_t(I - beta_t k_t k_t^T). Unlike Mamba2-style scalar transitions, these matrices do not commute, so a simple cumulative-sum trick over gates is not sufficient. TreeWY constructs a closed form over strict ancestor relationships in the draft tree and computes the outputs for all draft nodes with a triangular solve. It avoids storing a full recurrent-state snapshot per node. On commit, it reconstructs only the accepted state and uses a compact pseudo-value matrix as the transient object. Compared with ReplaySSM, the overlap is the triangular solve inside the verification window; the difference is that TreeWY directly targets tree verification rather than mainly chain-only deferred materialization.","experiments":"The provided content gives a detailed memory analysis rather than full throughput numbers. The examples are two Qwen3.5 hybrids: 35B-A3B with 30 GDN layers, 10 softmax layers, and 32 value heads; and 397B-A17B with 45 GDN layers, 15 softmax layers, and 64 value heads. Both use a 3:1 GDN-to-softmax ratio, d_k = d_v = 128 for GDN, softmax head dimension 256, and 2 KV heads. In normal decoding, the softmax KV cache costs about 20 KiB/token and 30 KiB/token, or 0.625 GiB/sequence and 0.94 GiB/sequence at 32K context. The committed GDN state is fixed at about 30 MiB/sequence and 90 MiB/sequence. With full-state snapshotting and k=3 draft tokens, verification needs four GDN states, about 120 MiB/sequence and 360 MiB/sequence; a tree with N nodes needs N+1 states and cannot share snapshots across branches. We instead learn inside the dequantization gap: with the integer codes and scales of a 4-bit release frozen, new knowledge is written only into the per-weight residual that lives strictly inside each quantization decision cell. Re-quantization then returns the released artif","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T08:49:07Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20873.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何在不改变已发布 4-bit 量化模型权重（保持 bit 级一致性）的前提下，对其进行更新（如知识注入或修正），以避免破坏已有的评估、认证和缓存。","importance":"提出了一种全新的模型更新范式，将量化误差视为可利用的存储空间。这对于部署在生产环境中的 LLM 极具启发，它允许在不破坏原有认证体系的情况下进行可逆的微调，为持续学习和模型编辑提供了一种极低成本的路径。","technical_approach":"提出 In-Cell Learning (CellFill)。核心思想是冻结 4-bit 量化模型的整数编码和缩放因子，仅在每个权重对应的量化决策单元（quantization decision cell，即量化时被舍弃的区间）内部，写入全精度的残差。重新量化时，由于残差被限制在单元内部，模型会逐位（bit-for-bit）还原为原始发布的量化模型。更新是完全可逆的（丢弃残差即可恢复原模型），且每个权重的偏移量被严格限制在已知半径内。","experiments":"在 Qwen3-1.7B 模型上，使用 1000 个合成事实进行实验。在严格保持量化不变的约束下，不同的约束实现路径能恢复 24.0% 到 61.4% 的事实。跨域困惑度（LAMBADA）随着知识吸收单调上升（从 28.67 升至 52.2），表明知识注入是有代价的。实验还发现，约束不仅限制了容量，还改变了学习动态。","reading_guide":"重点关注 Introduction 中对 In-Cell Learning 概念的定义，以及实验部分关于知识吸收率和困惑度权衡的分析。预计阅读时间 15 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to update a deployed 4-bit quantized LLM (e.g., for knowledge injection or correction) without changing its released weights (maintaining bit-identical consistency), thereby preserving existing evaluations, certifications, and caches.","importance":"Introduces a novel paradigm for model updating by treating quantization error as addressable storage. This is highly relevant for production-deployed LLMs, allowing for reversible fine-tuning without invalidating existing certification frameworks, offering a low-cost path for continual learning and model editing.","technical_approach":"Proposes In-Cell Learning (CellFill). The core idea is to freeze the integer codes and scaling factors of a 4-bit quantized model and write full-precision residuals strictly within the quantization decision cell of each weight (the interval discarded during quantization). Upon re-quantization, the model reproduces the original released artifact bit-for-bit. Updates are exactly revocable (dropping the residual restores the original model), and the excursion of each weight is strictly bounded by a known radius.","experiments":"Evaluated on Qwen3-1.7B using 1,000 synthetic facts. Under the strict invariance constraint, different constraint-realizing paths recover between 24.0% and 61.4% of the facts. It applies alternating closed-form left orthogonal rotation and manifold-optimized right orthogonal rotation to the retained weights. The difficulty is that agent execution graphs are induced by model outputs. Small decoding differences can change whether the agent calls a tool, branches, reflects, or stops, which then changes the number of LLM calls, prompt lengths, output lengths, and dependency structure. The paper shows this is not a minor effect: running the same request twice with mini-SWE-agent and gpt-oss-120b yields about 70% variation in the number of LLM calls on average. That makes conventional serving benchmarks, which assume independent requests or simple multi-turn chats, a poor proxy for real agent traffic.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for foundation-model researchers because it sharpens the interface between model capability and deployment cost. XPerf contributes a usable benchmark and instrumentation layer for that regime. Its main value is threefold: it turns nondeterministic agent behavior into a replayable workload; it exposes system and hardware counters that explain latency rather than just reporting it; and it provides a public, extensible setup instead of a proprietary trace collection. Yet large language models exhibit the lost-in-the-middle (LitM) effect: information near the center of a long context is retrieved less reliably than information near the edges. In clinical use this is not benign: the single most consequential fact in a note can sit at its center.
