In this paper, we introduce Thinkingbox, ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-20T07:37:57Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_19741.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.DB"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“agent 会不会调工具”，而是“agent 能不能在有持久后果的业务流程里稳定把事情办成”。现有很多 benchmark 主要检查答案表面是否合理、tool call 是否可执行，或者单步 API 选择是否正确，但对真实业务里更关键的部分覆盖不够：多轮澄清、策略约束、跨工具依赖、最终数据库状态是否正确、有没有多做或错做副作用。Thinkingbox 把这类任务形式化为一个可复现的 tool-agent-user 闭环环境，并把评测目标从‘轨迹像不像’转到‘终态和副作用对不对’。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，更接近 agent reliability 评测基础设施。但对基座研究仍有两点启发。第一，它把“成功一次”和“稳定成功”明确拆开，用 pass@1、pass@k、pass^k 去量化发现能力与可靠性之间的差距，这对理解模型在长链交互中的策略稳定性、状态跟踪能力、以及训练信号是否足够约束很有帮助。第二，它把评测对象从代码和网页扩展到 stateful business workflow，这类环境更接近企业内真实工具链，也更容易暴露语言模型在长期状态维护、确认流程、策略遵循上的短板。贡献量级主要在 benchmark 和 sandbox 设计，不在新模型或新训练范式。","technical_approach":"方法上分两层。第一层是 Thinkingbox sandbox。它把任务定义为一个有限时域的 POMDP：隐状态包含后端状态、模拟用户私有状态、评测相关事件日志和 episode 状态；agent 每一步只能看到对话历史和工具返回，动作是给用户发消息或调用某个工具及参数。工具调用在隔离的 backend session 中执行，系统记录 side effects，并在结束后运行隐藏的 executable checks。这个设计的关键不是把参考轨迹写死，而是允许多条有效路径，只要最终 backend state、必要副作用和对话约束满足即可。\n\n第二层是 Thinkingbox-bench。基于这个 sandbox，作者构建了 507 个任务，覆盖零售/电商、旅行与酒店、车险、新银行内部 IT 支持、咨询公司的 IT/HR 支持五个域。任务强调几类常见企业流程模式：先查记录再更新、需要用户补充信息、需要确认后才能执行不可逆操作、受 domain policy 约束的变更、以及多工具串联。评测上，绝大多数任务用终态 backend checks 判定，另有 30 个任务额外加了二值 rubric，检查最终回复是否包含必要披露、是否泄露不该说的信息、以及文字说明是否与实际执行结果一致。\n\n作者还专门强调 reliability 统计方式。pass@k 衡量多次尝试里是否至少成功一次，更像“发现能力”；pass^k 衡量 k 次是否次次成功，更接近“可依赖性”。这个区分很重要，因为很多 agent 在随机性、提示微扰或工具返回变化下会出现明显不稳定，单看 best-of-k 会高估真实可用性。","experiments":"实验核心结论很清楚：当前模型在这类 stateful workflow 上还有明显可靠性缺口。基准共 507 个任务，作者评测了 12 个 proprietary 和 open-weight 模型，并对每个任务重复运行 20 次。最强模型的 pass@1 为 65.36%，说明单次运行离可用还有距离；pass@20 达到 91.12%，说明很多任务并不是完全不会，而是偶尔能做对；但 pass^20 只有 25.25%，说明若要求 20 次都稳定成功，可靠性会大幅下滑。这个 discovery–reliability gap 是全文最重要的实验发现。\n\n从任务设计看，评测不是只看最终一句话，而是检查 terminal backend state，因此能区分几类常见失败：查对了对象但改错字段；回复里说会处理但后端没改；少做一步必要更新；多做了不该做的副作用；没拿到确认就执行高风险操作；以及最终话术与实际执行结果不一致。文中还做了 evaluator 相关分析和 failure mode 拆解，用来说明 fluent tool use 与 reliable work completion 不是一回事。\n\n摘要里没有给出更细的分域数字和逐模型排名细节，但从引言和结果概述已经足够看出 benchmark 的区分度：既不是简单 function calling，也不是纯对话任务，而是把状态正确性放在首位，因此能把模型的“偶然成功”与“稳定完成”拉开。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不需要逐式推导。建议先看 Introduction，抓住作者到底在批评现有 agent benchmark 的什么缺口。然后看 Thinkingbox sandbox 的定义和 Figure 2，理解 agent、simulated user、tool backend、side-effect extractor、judge 是怎么串起来的。接着重点看 benchmark 构成、任务检查方式，以及结果部分里 pass@1 / pass@20 / pass^20 的对比，这几处基本决定了这篇工作的价值。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看三块：1）任务判定为何采用 terminal state 而不是参考轨迹；2）为什么要把 reliability 单独定义成 pass^k；3）failure analysis 里哪些错误是语言能力问题，哪些是状态管理和策略执行问题。预计 25–35 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。若你做 agent training 或 tool-use post-training，可以再细看 rubric 设计和 evaluator ablation，因为这些会直接影响训练目标怎么定义。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a gap that many agent benchmarks leave open: not whether an agent can emit plausible tool calls, but whether it can reliably complete consequential, stateful business workflows. In these settings, success depends on multi-turn clarification, policy compliance, coordination across dependent tools, and ending in the correct persistent backend state without unintended side effects. Thinkingbox is built to evaluate that full loop rather than surface-level response quality or call validity.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper. expert-","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-20T15:13:41Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20153.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"把“LLM 做理论计算机科学研究”从零散能力测评（只测 autoformalization 或只测证明）推进到更接近真实研究流程的端到端评测：从论文语境里的自然语言核心主张出发，补齐 paper-specific 定义/假设/引理依赖，最终产出可在 Lean + Mathlib 顶会论文）和端到端分解式诊断结合起来，给出一个更难被训练集污染、也更能暴露模型短板的评测面。对预训练研究的启发点主要有两类：第一，当前模型在“数学建模/形式化建模”（把自然语言主张变成合适的形式化定义与定理陈述）上明显弱于“在给定形式化目标下做证明搜索”，这会影响后续是否值得在 pretrain 阶段引入更强的结构化/形式化语料与目标；第二，端到端分解评测能把能力缺口具体化为可训练的子任务（定义生成、陈述对齐、依赖管理），比单一成功率更利于设计数据配比与中期训练（mid-train）策略。","technical_approach":"数据与任务设计是核心。FormalTCS 由 175 个实例组成，每个实例来自一篇 2025–2026 年 STOC/FOCS/SODA/COLT 论文，保留论文特有的定义、假设与证明依赖结构；并由专家给出经验证的 Lean 4.32.2 + 对应 Mathlib 版本的形式化与证明。基准将“从自然语言到形式化证明”的过程拆成五个阶段逐段评测，从而能区分：是 autoformalization（定义/定理陈述构造）失败，还是证明阶段失败，还是依赖管理/分解策略失败。作者还在基准之上搭了一个端到端研究框架：让模型先提出主张，再尝试形式化并证明；结果显示模型更容易证明“自己提出的、自己表述的”主张，但这些主张经人工检查往往缺乏新意，指向所谓 research taste 的不足。与以往 TCS/数学基准相比，差异点在于：内容更新（前沿论文而非教材/Mathlib 现成定理）、语境更真实（paper-specific 设定与多层依赖）、评测更可诊断（分阶段而非单点能力）。","experiments":"基准规模 175。形式化证明复杂度统计：平均 22.0 条 statements、29.6 个 proof Modular configurations show specification-gaming rates as high as 47.9%, while no gaming is detected in the evaluated ARG runs. Example tasks include Stanford Cars ProMetaR, BTAD URD, ZINC NeuralWalker, ETTh1 AMD, California Housing Binary Diffusion, and CNN/Daily Mail summarization, illustrating substantial heterogeneity in codebases and workflows.","reading_guide":"Skim in 25–35 minutes. In the expert comparison, eight domain experts complete 53 prompts, and their pooled selections are statistically indistinguishable from indifferent choice. A sensitivity calculation bounds the detectable expert preference map at about 7.5% of the average model’s. score","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T18:00:04Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_19323.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是黑盒 LLM 的置信度估计，而不是再发明一个新的零样本 uncertainty score。更具体地说，作者关心部署场景里一个更实际的问题：既然上线前通常已经有一批带标注的目标任务样本，能不能把这些样本用起来，把原本只靠启发式分数或多次采样得到的“不确定性分数”转成更准确、也更校准的“回答正确概率”。论文把问题明确成监督式 correctness prediction：输入是 query、LLM response、已有 UQ 分数，以及与参考样本相似邻居的统计量；输出是该回答为真的概率。","importance":"对基座预训练本身，它不是训练范式论文，但对 pretrain→deployment 接口有直接启发。第一，它提醒人们很多 LLM UQ 工作停留在 zero-shot score 设计，而真实部署里往往已经有任务分布上的标注集，忽略这部分信息会白白损失可用信号。第二，它把“heuristic uncertainty score”和“confidence estimate”区分开，强调后者更适合决策、拒答和风险控制，这一点对后续 reward 建模、verifier、self-evaluation 也有共通意义。第三，方法几乎不依赖模型内部 logits 或 hidden states，适合闭源 API 模型，因此在实际系统里可迁移性较强。贡献量级不在于提出新的理论框架，而在于把一个常被默认忽略的监督校准步骤做成了简单、便宜、稳定增益的 pipeline。","technical_approach":"方法核心很朴素：把 LLM 回答是否正确当成二分类任务来学。作者先假设有一个固定黑盒 LLM 和一个目标任务数据集，并将数据拆成 training set 与 reference set。对每个 query-response 对，先计算已有的 UQ 分数作为基础特征；这些分数可以来自 verbalized confidence、self-consistency 类分数、token-level 或 generation-level 启发式分数，也可以是多个分数的组合。然后再利用 reference set 做邻域特征工程：在 embedding 空间里为当前 query 找 k 个最近邻，提取这些邻居的 correctness label、相似度、邻域正确率等统计量。这样，模型不只是看“当前样本自己有多不确定”，还看“和它相似的问题上模型通常会不会错”。\n\n在学习器上，作者没有执着于单一模型，而是比较了多种低成本分类器，并把 task-dependent learner selection 当成结论的一部分。输出目标是 correctness probability，因此 calibration 是评估重点之一。文中还讨论了 temperature scaling 这类后处理校准方法，但这里的主线不是对单个 logit 做温度缩放，而是直接学习一个从多源特征到正确率概率的映射。这个设计和传统零样本 UQ 的差别在于：零样本方法默认 uncertainty score 本身就足够表达风险；这里则承认 score 只是 feature，真正的置信度需要结合任务分布和局部相似样本来学习。","experiments":"从全文给出的结论看，作者在多个模型与数据集上都观察到一致提升：监督式分类器几乎总能优于原始 UQ 分数，且能给出更好的 calibration。实验重点不只是 accuracy/AUROC 一类区分能力，还包括 ECE 这类校准指标，这和论文强调“概率可用性”是对齐的。结果层面，作者反复强调两点：一是增益具有一致性，不依赖某一种基础 uncertainty score；二是最优分类器随任务变化，没有一个 learner 在所有数据分布上都最好，这也是他们反对纯 zero-shot UQ 的原因之一。\n\n从方法消融上，最关键的信号来自两类特征：已有 UQ 分数本身，以及 reference-neighbourhood 统计。前者提供样本级不确定性线索，后者提供局部经验风险。论文还强调额外计算开销很低：训练的是轻量分类器，推理时只是在 LLM 生成之后做一次特征计算与分类，边际成本远低于重新采样多次生成。给定你提供的全文片段里没有完整数表，我不编造具体 AUROC/ECE 数字；但从作者写法看，held-out 数据上的 calibration 改善和 ranking 改善都是稳定结论，而不是个别任务偶然成立。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Method，就能抓住论文真正的主张：不是设计新分数，而是把部署前已有标注数据纳入 UQ。然后看 calibration 背景部分，确认作者为什么坚持把“正确概率”作为目标，而不是只做 abstention score。实验部分建议重点看三类表：1）原始 UQ 分数 vs 监督增强后的整体对比；2）不同分类器在不同任务上的差异；3）是否加入邻域特征的消融。阅读时间大约 20–30 分钟。\n\n如果你做 pretrain 或 post-train 接口研究，值得多想两个点。第一，这个框架其实是在做 task-conditional verifier calibration，和 reward model calibration、judge calibration 很接近。第二，它依赖“相似问题的历史正确率”这一局部统计，说明很多所谓 uncertainty 并不完全是模型内生信号，而是任务分布上的经验风险，这对构建更可靠的 self-evaluation 系统有启发。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles confidence estimation for black-box LLMs, not by inventing yet another zero-shot uncertainty score, but by asking a more deployment-relevant question: if a labeled in-domain dataset already exists before deployment, can that supervision be used to convert heuristic uncertainty signals into better estimates of response correctness? The task is formulated as supervised prediction of whether an LLM answer is correct, using existing UQ scores plus neighborhood statistics from similar labeled queries.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for the pretrain-to-deployment interface. Standard evaluation only verifies final label correctness, ignoring whether judgment changes stem from valid evidence, consistent rules, or proper rule applicability. We formalize evaluator reasoning accountability via three core sources: grounds, norms, and authority. Varying these sources yields an eight-cell counterfactual judgme","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T10:01:58Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20938.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决的是版本化 AI evaluator 的“判决变化可归因”问题。一个 evaluator 从旧版本到新版本，最终标签可能仍然正确，但原因可能错了；也可能标签发生变化，却无法说明变化来自证据、规则，还是规则适用权限。普通 accuracy、pairwise agreement、自由文本 rationale 都无法检测这种错误。问题在于：当 evaluator 的输出用于放行 agent 行为、分配人工审核、或回流为训练奖励时，错误的理由会污染后续决策和训练信号。","importance":"对预训练和后训练的影响主要在 evaluator 作为训练信号入口这一层。当前很多 RLHF、RLAIF、verifier、judge model、agent gating 系统只记录最终偏好或通过/拒绝标签，但没有验证标签变化是否由声明的证据或规则变化导致。这项工作给出一个可执行的归因单元：不是要求模型写一段解释，而是要求列出哪些源替换足以复现新判决。贡献量级不在模型能力提升，而在评测协议和反馈信号质量控制。它提醒训练系统不要只看 judge 的最终分数，还要检查 judge 在规则版本、证据版本、权限边界变化时是否保持因果一致。对于 reward hacking、verifier drift、数据回流污染，这是一类更细的检测工具。","technical_approach":"方法把 evaluator 的局部状态拆成三类有类型的来源：grounds 表示事实和证据，norms 表示决策规则，authority 表示规则是否适用于当前 case。旧状态和新状态之间，每一类来源都可以取旧版或新版，因此形成 2^3 个混合状态，称为 judgment cube。对每个混合状态执行可信 adjudicator，得到对应判决。若某个来源替换集合足以让旧状态复现新判决，并且没有更小的子集也能做到，则它是一个 inclusion-minimal sufficient replacement。所有这样的最小替换集合组成 judgment receipt。核心区分很重要：模型生成的 receipt 只是预测，只有把 counterfactual hybrid 真正送入可信 adjudicator 执行后，receipt 才能被认证。工作还引入 paired consistency。每个控制变换都带有已知输出关系，类似软件测试里的 metamorphic relation。若一对样本违反关系，就能确定至少一个预测错误，从而在没有变换后 gold label 的情况下得到错误率下界。这个设计比普通 robustness accuracy 更强，因为它检查的是变化关系，而不是孤立标签。","experiments":"实验资产是 ReasonBench，包含 45 条经审计的组织政策条款和 800 个独立生成的逻辑世界。每个样本都有可执行的旧状态、新状态、八个 hybrid 状态，以及精确的 receipt family。source unit 在 prompt rendering 前已经拆分，paired control 的标签由构造保证。关键结果不是单纯准确率，而是准确率和理由一致性的分离。作者预先冻结过一个假设：用 full-cube supervision 监督八个 cell 会优于直接监督 receipt。结果相反，full-cube supervision 没赢。原因是八个 cell 的联合预测比 receipt 本身更难，密集监督没有自动转化为更好的归因。更有信息量的失败包括：模型在测试准确率上接近 ceiling，但在 source reordering 下的一致性只有约 50%； revised verdict 在 composition shift 下相对稳定，但 receipt 不稳定。这说明 evaluator 可以学会给出对的最终判决，却无法稳定说明是哪类来源变化导致了判决。","reading_guide":"建议精读形式化定义和 ReasonBench 构造，而不是只看总体分数。重点看三处：第一，grounds、norms、authority 的类型化拆分，以及 judgment cube 和 judgment receipt 的定义，这是核心抽象；第二，paired consistency 和 Proposition 5，它说明为什么不需要变换后 gold label 也能得到错误下界；第三，实验里的 negative result，尤其是 full-cube supervision 失败、source reordering consistency 约 50%、verdict 与 receipt 在 composition shift 下表现分离。这些结果对训练 judge/verifier 很有用：要把最终标签正确性和原因归因正确性拆开评估。若用于 LLM 训练系统，可以进一步思考如何把 receipt certification 接入 reward model 更新、policy patching、或数据回流过滤。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work targets attribution for versioned AI evaluators. An evaluator may keep the correct final label while changing for the wrong reason, or it may change its verdict without making clear whether the change came from evidence, rules, or rule applicability. Final-label accuracy, agreement metrics, and free-form rationales do not expose this failure. The risk is practical: these evaluators often gate agent actions, route cases to human review, and feed labels back into training as reward or supervision.","importance":"The main relevance to foundation-model training is signal quality. Modern RLHF, RLAIF, verifier training, judge models, and agent control loops often treat evaluator outputs as scalar labels. This work argues that scalar correctness is too weak when the evaluator itself is versioned. A reward or verifier signal should not only be right; its change should be traceable to a declared change in evidence, rule, or authority. The contribution is an evaluation and certification protocol rather than a new model. It gives a concrete way to detect evaluator drift, inertia, and misattribution before those errors are recycled into training data or automated decisions.","technical_approach":"$33","experiments":"The benchmark, ReasonBench, contains 45 audited organizational policy clauses and 800 independently generated logical worlds. Each item has executable old and revised states, all eight hybrid states, and the exact receipt family. Source units are split before prompt rendering, and paired controls are labeled by construction. The most useful empirical result is the gap between verdict accuracy and reason stability. A pre-registered hypothesis that full-cube supervision would help did not hold: supervising all eight cells is a harder target than predicting the receipt, and denser supervision did not translate into better attribution. The failures are more informative than the headline score. Models can reach near-ceiling test accuracy while dropping to roughly 50% consistency under source reordering. Revised verdicts remain more stable under composition shifts than receipts do. The result shows that correct judgments and correct accounts of judgment change are separable capabilities.","reading_guide":"Read the formal sections carefully. of","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T17:59:52Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21360.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"评测“实时视频助手型”Omni-LLM 轮）覆盖多能力组合；用户指令以音频嵌入视频，输出为开放式文本；评分协议会惩罚冗余与幻觉。","experiments":"作者报告了一个总体分数对比：Gemini-3-Pro 66.4/100；开源 Qwen3-Omni-Instruct 51.2/100。并总结了四类主要瓶颈：\n1) 视觉提示跟随不足（如手势）；\n2) 长期记忆差，上下文窗口在几分钟内被耗尽；\n3) omni-modal LLMs as real-time video assistants is hard because interaction paths diverge: the assistant’s response changes the user’s next action, which changes subsequent video inputs. Static offline QA benchmarks cannot capture this closed-loop dynamic. The paper addresses how to build a reproducible, scorable benchmark that still reflects assistant-style multi-turn video interactions.","importance":"Primarily an evaluation contribution, with indirect but useful feedback to pretraining: it operationalizes assistant-style bottlenecks (visual prompt following, long-term memory under context limits, delayed response, cross-turn goal retention). These failure modes often trace back to pretraining/mid-training data formats and objectives, not only post-training. If you build omni-modal or long-context pretraining pipelines, the benchmark’s taxonomy can guide what data and objectives are missing.","technical_approach":"OmniAssistBench fixes interaction-path divergence by introducing explicit video-derived priors.\n- Reverse-engineer Internet videos: select suitable source videos, infer user goals and a standard procedural path (priors), then segment videos into multi-turn clips to simulate step-by-step assistant interactions.\n- During evaluation, provide the priors to the model and require guidance along a single route, reducing dependence on the model’s internal world knowledge and avoiding the need to dynamically generate new video branches.\n- Two-tier framework: Basic tier for foundational interactive perception/instruction-following (e.g., gestures, social relations); Advanced tier for goal-oriented tasks using everyday plot templates.\n- Additionally, film three long cases (~15 turns each) to test combined abilities; user instructions are embedded as audio; open-ended scoring penalizes redundancy and hallucinations.","experiments":"Reported headline results: Gemini-3-Pro scores 66.4/100; open-source Qwen3-Omni-Instruct scores 51.2/100. More detailed verification requires the full paper’s per-skill breakdown, scoring rubric, annotator agreement, and baseline coverage.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (30–45 minutes, benchmark-oriented):\n- Data construction: how priors are defined and presented; this determines whether the benchmark measures assistant reasoning or script regurgitation.\n- Scoring protocol: how open-ended answers are graded, how hallucination/redundancy penalties work, and inter-rater reliability.\n- Task taxonomy and examples: map each failure mode to likely training gaps (gesture-following data, temporal “wait” behavior, long-context training, cross-turn goal tracking).\n- The three long cases: useful for debugging context budgeting, goal persistence, and delayed-response behavior.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"针对Omni-LLM交互能力的评测，通过逆向工程视频构建交互路径的思路比较巧妙，值得关注其具体的评测指标设计。","en":"A benchmark for Omni-LLM interactive capabilities. However, most existing benchmarks are limited to the document level and focus mainly on high-resource languages, leaving many fine-grained challenges insufficiently evaluated. To address this gap, we present MGAL, the first multilingual, granularity- and position-aware long-context benchmark. MGAL is constructed from United Nations (UN) reports spanning 8K to 128K tokens across the six official UN languages. It covers ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T08:19:00Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20853.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“再做一个长上下文榜单”，而是把长上下文能力拆开测清楚。现有 benchmark 多半停留在文档级任务，语言覆盖偏高资源语种，也很少控制证据在长文中的位置，因此很难判断模型到底是不会跨粒度理解，还是只是被位置偏置、表面词重叠、评分方式等因素干扰。MGAL 试图把这几个变量显式分离：同一套长文材料上，同时考察词、句、段、文档四个粒度，并按 begin/middle/end 控制证据位置，再扩展到联合国六种官方语言。它要回答的是：长上下文模型在不同语言、不同粒度、不同证据位置下，失效模式到底长什么样。","importance":"对基座研究的价值主要在评测设计，而不是提出新模型。它补上了两个长期缺口。第一，长上下文评测过去偏“粗粒度”，容易把很多局部检索、句法衔接、段落功能理解的问题淹没在文档级分数里；MGAL 把能力分解后，更适合诊断训练目标、位置编码、长序列注意力和数据配比带来的具体短板。第二，它把多语言和位置控制做到了同一框架里，这对分析长上下文 scaling 是否只在英语成立、以及低资源语言为何掉队，很有帮助。对做预训练的人来说，这类 benchmark 的意义不在 leaderboard，而在于它能把“模型为什么错”暴露得更具体：是 recency bias、option-order bias、局部语义拥挤，还是生成时的 input anchoring 不足。","technical_approach":"MGAL 的核心做法是用联合国数字图书馆中的长篇报告构造一个位置对齐、跨语言可比的长上下文测试集。数据覆盖阿拉伯语、中文、英语、法语、俄语、西班牙语，输入长度从 8K 到 128K token。任务设计上分成 closed-ended 和 open-ended 两类，并覆盖四层语言粒度：词级、句级、段级、文档级。文中提到的任务包括 word-level QA、sentence-level cloze、paragraph filling、document summarization、translation 等，共 7 个任务；每种语言每个任务有 420 个 query-response 对。关键不只是任务种类，而是构造时同时控制了证据位置：在文档级和段落级都标注 begin/middle/end，使得同一模型在不同位置上的退化可以被直接比较。\n\n数据来源选 UN 报告有两个原因。一个是天然多语平行和主题一致，便于做跨语言对照；另一个是文档结构较规范，适合定义词、句、段、文档四层 coherent unit。作者强调 same-position sentences 在六种语言中语义对齐，这让跨语言比较不只是“题型类似”，而是材料位置和语义都尽量一致。质量控制上，所有 query-response pair 都经过人工审查并删除有缺陷样本，明显是偏小而精的 benchmark 路线。\n\n评分方面，closed-ended 任务主要用 Accuracy，开放生成任务用 ROUGE-L 等 reference-based 指标；对于 paragraph filling 这类仅靠 n-gram overlap 很难评准的任务，又引入了 LLM-as-a-judge。这个设计比较务实：如果只用自动指标，会低估语义正确但表述不同的答案；如果全靠 judge，又会把 benchmark 的可复现性变差。MGAL 采取的是两者并用。\n\n从方法贡献看，它不是提出新的评测算法，而是把三个维度系统化了：multilingual、granularity-aware、position-aware。和 LongBench、LV-Eval、M4LE、ONERULER 这类已有工作相比，区别不在规模更大，而在控制变量更细，能支持更明确的误差归因。","experiments":"实验部分的重点不是谁第一，而是错误模式。作者评测了 12 个 long-context LLM。摘要和正文给出的主要结论有四类。\n\n第一，粒度越粗，性能越差。模型在词级任务上普遍表现较好，但从句子、段落到文档，能力明显下降。这说明很多模型的“长上下文能力”更接近局部匹配或局部检索，而不是稳定的跨段组织与全篇整合。\n\n第二，多语言差异在低资源语言上被放大。大开源模型和闭源模型在高资源语言上的差距没有想象中大，但到了低资源语言，闭源模型优势更稳定，小型开源模型掉得更明显。这个现象对预训练很关键，因为它暗示长上下文能力并不是单独由架构决定，语料覆盖和跨语言表示质量仍然是主导因素。\n\n第三，位置敏感性依然明显。sentence-cloze 中，空缺出现在文本前部时错误率最高，越靠后越好，和 recency-biased attention 的已有观察一致。作者还发现明显的 early-option bias：模型更容易选前面的选项，如 A/B，而不是逐项核对证据。这说明一部分错误不是“不会读长文”，而是推理过程被答题格式诱导。\n\n第四，作者提出了两个更细的失效现象。其一是 local semantic crowding：相邻句子共享大量实体、年份、连接词时，模型容易根据表面重叠选错句子，忽略句子在段落中的功能角色，比如背景、解释、结果。其二是 fluency-consistency gap：生成结果往往流畅、风格也对，但和输入锚定不够，容易出现 factual drift 或无依据补写。这个观察对长上下文生成很重要，因为很多模型在 summarization/filling 上看起来“像对了”，但其实证据绑定很弱。\n\n从数据规模上，benchmark 覆盖 6 种语言、7 个任务、4 个粒度、上下文长度 8K–128K，每语言每任务 420 个样本。虽然正文截断处没有完整表格数字，但这些设置已经足够支撑细粒度切片分析。若做复现实验，最值得关注的是不同模型在 begin/middle/end 和不同 granularity 上的相对斜率，而不是单一总分。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不需要逐段精读。建议先看 Introduction 和任务定义表，确认它到底比 LongBench 一类工作多控制了哪些变量。然后重点看 benchmark construction：数据来源、跨语言位置对齐、人工审核流程，这决定了评测结论是否可信。实验部分优先看按 granularity 和 position 分层的主结果图，而不是总榜单；真正有信息量的是性能如何随词→句→段→文档退化，以及 begin/middle/end 的差异曲线。再看作者对 local semantic crowding 和 fluency-consistency gap 的案例分析，这两部分最接近“为什么模型会错”。如果时间有限，30–40 分钟足够抓住主要信息。若你在做长上下文预训练或位置编码，值得把相关图表和错误案例细看一遍，因为它们能直接转化成训练诊断假设。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not trying to add another long-context leaderboard. Its actual target is diagnosis. Existing benchmarks mostly evaluate document-level tasks, focus on high-resource languages, and rarely control where the supporting evidence appears in the context. That makes it hard to tell whether a model fails because it cannot reason across discourse units, because it is position-biased, or because the task setup hides the failure mode. MGAL isolates these factors explicitly: it evaluates word-, sentence-, paragraph-, and document-level understanding, stratifies evidence by begin/middle/end positions, and does so across the six official UN languages. The central question is: what exactly breaks in long-context LLMs when granularity, language, and evidence position are varied in a controlled way?","importance":"Its value is in benchmark design rather than model innovation. Two gaps are addressed. First, long-context evaluation has been too coarse. Document-level scores often hide failures in local retrieval, sentence function discrimination, paragraph coherence, and evidence anchoring. By decomposing performance by granularity, MGAL is more useful for diagnosing effects of pretraining data mix, positional schemes, long-sequence attention, and instruction tuning. Second, it puts multilinguality and position control into the same framework. That matters for understanding whether long-context scaling trends are mostly English artifacts and why lower-resource languages lag more sharply. For pretraining researchers, the benchmark matters less as a ranking tool and more as a failure-surface probe: recency bias, option-order bias, local semantic crowding, and weak grounding in generation become visible as separate phenomena.","technical_approach":"$34","experiments":"$35","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, not one that requires line-by-line reading. Start with the introduction and the task overview table to see exactly which variables MGAL controls beyond prior long-context benchmarks. Then read the benchmark construction section carefully: data source, multilingual position alignment, and manual auditing are what determine whether the later claims are trustworthy. In the experiments, focus on results broken down by granularity and position rather than the overall leaderboard. The most useful plots are the degradation curves across discourse units and the begin/middle/end slices. After that, read the qualitative analysis on local semantic crowding and the fluency-consistency gap; those sections contain the most actionable insights for model development. If time is limited, 30–40 minutes should be enough to extract the main value. We introduce ProofJudge, an agentic LLM-as-judge system that scores formal proof quality along five dimensions beyond correctness: library leverage, automation fit, structural clarity, statement quality, and Mathlib conventions. We evaluate ProofJudge on a novel dataset of 218 declarations drawn from distinct Mathlib PRs. t","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-20T02:39:40Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20432.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LO","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的不是“证明是否正确”，而是“通过 Lean kernel 检查的证明，是否达到了 Mathlib 可合入的质量标准”。在形式化数学里，正确性只是底线；真正影响库可维护性和后续复用价值的，是是否善用现有库、是否写出可复用的 lemma、结构是否清楚、是否符合社区约定。论文要解决的是：能否用带工具访问的 LLM judge，稳定地复现 Mathlib 人类 reviewer 对 proof quality 的偏好，而不是只做编译通过/不通过的二元判断。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接贡献不大，但对“可验证环境中的高质量奖励建模”有明确启发。Lean 证明生成近来常用 verifiable reward，只奖励 type-check correctness；这会把模型推向“能过 kernel 但人不想合”的方向。ProofJudge 提供了一个更接近真实维护者偏好的软质量信号，适合作为后续 RL、rejection sampling 或 self-improvement 的奖励源。更重要的是，它说明在工具接地后，LLM 可以读出代码审查里那些难以形式化但又真实存在的标准。不过这篇工作的量级仍偏评测与 judge 设计，不是预训练范式层面的推进。","technical_approach":"方法核心是一个 agentic LLM-as-judge。输入不是单纯的 proof text，而是某个 Mathlib PR 中同一 declaration 的两个版本：早期被要求修改的版本，以及最终被合入的版本。judge 在评估单个版本时并不知道另一个版本的分数，也不知道自己正在做 pairwise 比较，从而避免显式比较带来的提示泄漏。评分 rubric 被拆成五个维度：library leverage、automation fit、structural clarity、statement quality、Mathlib conventions，每个维度打 1 到 10 分，再由 harness 加权成总分。关键设计在于 tool grounding：judge 可以通过 bash 查询 PR 所对应 commit 的 Mathlib 状态，搜索现有定理、查看签名、检查库中已有 API，从而像人类 reviewer 一样判断“你是不是重复造轮子”“这个 lemma 是否应该更一般化”“是否已有一行可调用的现成结果”。这和只靠静态文本 judge 的区别很大，因为 proof quality 很多时候依赖库上下文，而不是 proof token 本身。数据集构造上，作者先用 Claude Sonnet 5 回顾历史 PR，筛出那些 revision 差异显著、且不是纯 linting 或新增 declaration 的样本，再形成 dev split 和 test split。这个筛法有一定人工启发式成分，但能把评测集中在真正涉及 reviewer judgment 的案例上。","experiments":"评测集包含 218 个 declaration pair，另有 123 个 dev pair 用于 rubric 调整。作者测试了 6 个 judge，含 3 个开源权重和 3 个闭源模型，每个模型做 3 次 replicate；每次评估时每个样本最多允许 20 次工具调用，以控制成本。指标是 preference alignment：judge 是否给最终被 Mathlib 接受的 revision 更高分。结果上，所有模型都显著高于 50% 随机基线。最好的是 claude-sonnet-5，达到 80.8%；较弱的 deepseek-v4-flash 也有 63.5%，sign test 的显著性都达到 p < 1e-5。开源模型里，muse-glimmer-30b 为 70.2%，inkling-small 为 69.3%，说明这件事并不完全依赖最强闭源模型。更有价值的是作者对噪声的分析：同一个 judge 在同一批样本上重复运行，约五分之一到接近一半的 verdict 会翻转；某些 ablation 在一次 replicate 上 p=0.34，另一次却是 p=0.006。这说明 judge 方差很大，单次跑分很容易误判方法差异。文中给出的 PR 11640 案例也很说明问题：初版证明重复构造了库里已有结果，修订版直接调用 H.restrictPreimage s；judge 通过搜索库发现已有 declaration，并在 library leverage 维度稳定压低初版分数。这类判断无法靠 lint 或正则完成，必须依赖语义级工具使用。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点看三处。第一是方法部分对五维 rubric 的定义，以及为什么需要 tool grounding；这是整篇工作的真正方法点。第二是结果部分关于 replicate variance 的讨论，这比主表分数更重要，直接关系到以后把 judge 当 reward 是否会导致训练不稳。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not about proof correctness per se. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-20T10:16:07Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2608_19863.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["eess.AS","cs.AI","cs.SD"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题很明确：音频自监督学习长期依赖较重的训练配方，例如重建解码器、离散声学 tokenizer、EMA teacher、额外正则或多码本量化，训练目标和系统复杂度都偏高；而语言和视觉里已经出现了更简单的“预测下一个 token / embedding”范式。作者想验证，音频这种天然按时间展开的模态，是否可以直接用因果式 next-patch embedding prediction 做预训练，并在不引入复杂辅助机制的前提下学到可迁移、可扩展的表征。更具体地说，难点不只是把 spectrogram 切成 patch 再做自回归，而是如何在二维时频网格上线性化、如何定义稳定的连续目标预测损失、以及这种目标是否真的能兼顾音频事件识别、语音命令和情感等下游任务。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它把“next-embedding prediction”从视觉延伸到音频，而且音频比图像更适合因果顺序建模，因为时间轴是真实生成顺序，不是人为指定的扫描顺序。第二，它提供了一个更接近统一多模态 LM 接口的音频预训练方案：目标不是重建像素级谱图，也不是依赖外部离散 tokenizer，而是直接预测连续 patch embedding，这和视觉中的 JEPA/next-latent 路线、以及语言中的 next-token 训练在接口上更接近。第三，它对工程上有现实价值：如果结果成立，音频 SSL 可以用更少组件获得有竞争力的迁移性能，这对后续做统一音频-语言、音频-视频、甚至 AR 多模态预训练都有启发。不过它的贡献更偏“范式验证 + 设计空间梳理”，不是从根本上改写音频建模框架。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 NAPE。输入先转成 log-mel spectrogram，再切成不重叠 patch，经线性投影得到二维 patch embedding 网格。随后作者不做 masked reconstruction，也不引入 target encoder，而是把二维网格按某种扫描顺序展平成一维序列，送入因果 Transformer。模型在位置 t 只能看见前缀 patch，预测下一个 patch 的 embedding。训练损失采用对目标 embedding stop-gradient 后的 negative cosine similarity，而不是 L2 回归或离散分类。这个选择有两个考虑：一是避免 embedding 范数主导训练，二是让目标空间保持连续，不需要额外 tokenizer。\n\n全文里最值得注意的是“扫描顺序”被当成音频特有的重要设计轴。图像里 raster scan 往往只是实现细节，但音频谱图的时间轴和频率轴语义不同，线性化方式会直接改变可见上下文和归纳偏置。作者系统比较了四种扫描策略，核心是在“优先沿时间推进”还是“交替覆盖时频局部结构”之间做权衡。直觉上，若顺序过度打散时间连续性，模型虽然仍是因果的，但学到的就不再是自然声学演化；若顺序只沿时间推进，又可能牺牲局部时频模式的建模效率。论文的一个主要技术贡献就是把这个问题显式化，并用实验说明不同扫描方式对迁移性能和训练行为有实质影响。\n\n模型本身保持极简：单塔 causal encoder + 轻量 predictor head，没有 BYOL/EMA 式双分支，没有重建 decoder，也没有额外 variance/covariance regularizer。这样的设计与现有音频 SSL 的区别很清楚：它不把预训练看成“恢复被遮挡内容”或“对齐 teacher 表征”，而是直接把音频当作顺序生成过程来建模。这个取向更接近语言模型，也更容易和其他模态的 AR/next-latent 训练统一。","experiments":"实验覆盖 AudioSet 预训练，以及 ESC-50、Speech Commands V1/V2、IEMOCAP 等下游评测。模型规模给了三档：Small 19M、Base 85M、Large 303M，重点是看这种简单目标是否具备规模扩展性。根据正文描述，NAPE 在多个任务上达到或接近当时最优水平，且不仅微调表现强，线性探针也有竞争力，这一点比较关键，因为 next-embedding 目标并不直接优化线性可分性。\n\n消融部分的价值高于单点榜单。作者系统考察了：1）不同扫描顺序；2）模型规模；3）训练目标与结构细节。结论大致是，顺序设计确实重要，尊重时间因果结构的扫描方式通常更稳、更强；模型从 Small 扩到 Large 时性能持续提升，没有出现明显的目标失效；而在不使用 decoder、tokenizer、teacher 的情况下，简单 cosine prediction 已足以支撑有效表征学习。正文还给了注意力图和 embedding 空间可视化，显示模型会形成与声学事件边界、局部时频模式相关的结构。由于给到的全文片段没有完整表格数字，这里不编造具体百分点；但可以确定的是，作者把“简单目标是否够用”这个问题做成了较完整的实证论证，而不是只给一个最好结果。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 Introduction 和方法图，先把问题意识抓住：作者不是在做又一个 masked spectrogram model，而是在测试音频是否适合 next-embedding 预训练。然后重点看扫描顺序定义那一节，这是全文最有信息量的设计点。接着看主结果表，关注两件事：一是和依赖复杂 recipe 的音频 SSL 方法相比，性能差距到底有多大；二是线性探针是否也成立。最后看消融和注意力分析，判断这个方法学到的是不是稳定的时序声学结构，而不是偶然的优化产物。\n\n如果时间有限，30 分钟左右足够抓住主线：方法图、扫描顺序实验、主表、一个可视化章节。若要判断它对统一多模态预训练有没有启发，则需要更仔细看 patch 线性化和损失设计，因为这两点决定它能否自然接到更通用的 next-latent 框架里。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a clean question: can audio SSL be built around a simple causal next-embedding objective, instead of the increasingly heavy recipes common in audio pretraining? Existing approaches often rely on reconstruction decoders, acoustic tokenizers, EMA teachers, auxiliary regularizers, or multi-codebook quantization. The authors test whether audio, which is intrinsically sequential along time, can support a language-model-like training interface: predict the next spectrogram patch embedding from previous patches. The real challenge is not just patchifying a spectrogram. It is how to linearize a 2D time-frequency grid into a causal sequence, how to define a stable continuous prediction target, and whether such a minimalist objective can still produce transferable representations across audio and speech tasks.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is threefold. First, it brings next-embedding prediction into audio, where the modality is arguably better aligned with causal modeling than images because temporal order is native rather than imposed. Second, it moves audio SSL closer to a unified multimodal pretraining interface: no reconstruction to raw mel bins, no external discrete tokenizer, just prediction in latent space. That makes it conceptually closer to next-token LM and to recent latent-prediction work in vision. Third, it is useful from a systems and recipe perspective. If competitive audio representations can be learned with a single causal encoder and a simple cosine loss, then audio pretraining becomes easier to scale and easier to integrate into broader multimodal AR or next-latent frameworks. The contribution is best read as a strong paradigm validation and design-space study, not as a wholesale replacement of all existing audio SSL methods.","technical_approach":"$3d","experiments":"$3e","reading_guide":"$3f","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读目标函数和消融即可；它测试“音频是否也能吃 next-token prior”，结论若扎实会影响 audio tokenizer 取舍。","en":"Read the objective and ablations; it tests whether audio can inherit the next-token prior, which matters for audio-tokenizer choices."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Umberto Cappellazzo","Xubo Liu","Stavros Petridis","Maja Pantic"],"notable_authors":["Maja Pantic","Stavros Petridis"],"tags":["audio-pretraining","autoregressive-modeling","self-supervised-learning","continuous-embeddings"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19863","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.19863","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.19726","title":"Projector Is All You Train","abstract":"The typical training process of a multimodal large language model (MLLM) involves adapting both the language model backbone and the projector between the backbone and a modality-specific encoder. 将这些特征映射到 LM token embedding 维度；projector 固定为 3 层 MLP，隐藏维度为 1024 和 2048，中间使用 GELU。点云 token 被放在新增的 <|pc3d_start|> 和 <|pc3d_end|> 输入标记之间。训练目标仍是 causal language modeling，但使用 response-only SFT，只对 assistant 每 step 开销更高，按 step 对齐会给它更多实际算力。结果显示，projector-only 在 3D 分类和 captioning benchmark 上相对已有 3D MLLM baseline 表现强，并且在相同 encoder、backbone 和算力预算下，与 projector+LoRA 联合训练持续保持竞争力。效率上，projector-only 的训练样本吞吐约为 joint training 的 2 倍。能力保持方面，joint training 在语言、视觉和空间推理 benchmark 上出现回退；projector-only 因为不更新 backbone，结构上避免了这类参数漂移。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 15–25 分钟。先看 Introduction 中的问题设定和结论边界：结论主要来自 3D 的关键成本。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether adapting an MLLM to a new modality really requires updating the language-model backbone, or whether training only the modality projector is enough.","importance":"The useful pretraining-related lesson is the separation between modality alignment and language-model modification. In the 3D MLLM setting, a trained projector can provide strong multimodal performance without changing the LM weights. This supports a conservative route for adding modalities to existing LMs: map the new encoder representation into the LM embedding space first, and update the backbone only when the evidence justifies the drift risk. It also makes clear that multimodal adaptation should be evaluated not only by new-modality accuracy, but also by regressions in existing language, vision, and spatial reasoning capabilities.","technical_approach":"The architecture contains a frozen point-cloud encoder, a projector, and a pretrained decoder-only LM. The encoder produces a sequence of point features. A 3-layer MLP projector with hidden dimensions 1024 and 2048, using GELU activations, maps these features into the LM embedding dimension. The projected point tokens are inserted between two input-only special tokens, <|pc3d_start|> and <|pc3d_end|>. Training uses the standard causal language-modeling objective with response-only supervised fine-tuning, so only assistant-response tokens contribute to the loss. The comparison is between projector-only training, where only the projector is optimized, and joint training, where the projector and LoRA adapters on all linear layers of the LM are optimized. The data comes from the Objaverse subset of the mixed Stage 1 and Stage 2 data used by PointLLM-V2, without preserving the original two-stage training schedule.","experiments":"The study tests multiple LM backbones, including Qwen3.5-4B, Qwen3.5-9B, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, while keeping the 3D encoder fixed. Runs are matched by a 16-hour wall-clock budget on a single A100-80GB GPU rather than by optimizer steps, since joint training has higher per-step cost and would otherwise receive more actual compute. Projector-only models are competitive with joint projector-plus-LoRA training under the same encoder, backbone, data, and time budget. They also perform strongly relative to existing 3D MLLM baselines on 3D classification and captioning benchmarks. Projector-only training has roughly twice the training sample throughput of joint training. The drift analysis shows that joint training causes regressions on language, vision, and spatial reasoning evaluations, while projector-only training avoids backbone drift by construction.","reading_guide":"A useful skim should take about 15–25 minutes. Start with the introduction to understand the scope: the evidence is from 3D MLLMs and should not be automatically generalized to every modality or scale. In the method section, check the fairness of the comparison, especially wall-clock matching, where LoRA is inserted, the response-only loss, and the frozen-encoder setup. In the experiments, prioritize the training curves over final numbers alone; faster progress under the same time budget is central to the argument. The regression evaluations on existing capabilities are also important, because they expose the hidden cost of updating the LM backbone during multimodal adaptation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读消融和 drift 指标即可；它会削弱“新模态必须动 backbone”的默认做法。","en":"Read the ablations and drift metrics; it weakens the default assumption that a new modality requires backbone tuning."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Ramen VR","University of California, Berkeley","iph.so","Affiliation:Ramen VR","Affiliation:University of California, Berkeley","Affiliation:iph.so"],"authors":["Nyx Iskandar","Saathvik Selvan","Slater Victoroff"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["multimodal-adaptation","projector-tuning","3D-MLLM","capability-drift","modality-alignment"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19726","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.19726","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.20382","title":"Decoupled Vision-Language System for Multimodal Understanding and Generation","abstract":"We introduce a new architecture design for multimodal large language models (MLLMs), Libra, capable of both multimodal understanding and generation. multimodal modeling: when a single backbone is asked to do visual self-modeling, language self-modeling, cross-modal alignment, image generation, and text generation at once, training is easily skewed by modality imbalance. Text is abundant and semantically dense; image token sequences are longer and statistically very different. A fully shared computation path often over-favors language or reduces vision to a weak auxiliary channel. Libra tries to separate intra-modal modeling from cross-modal interaction without falling back to a pure cascade design.","importance":"From a foundation-model perspective, this is closer to native multimodal pretraining and architecture design than to standard VLM finetuning. Libra sits between unified backbones and cascade systems. DPO for discrete language models mostly reweights completed outputs, while preference optimization for flow or diffusion models changes the generative trajectory itself. That distinction matters for diffusion language models, flow-based language models, continuous latent reasoning, and unified AR-diffusion training. The main lesson is that a reference model or a pairwise preference objective does not automatically preserve the pretrained generative prior. A sample is counted as on-manifold if it lies in the surface domain and satisfies |z−f(x,y)|≤0.15. optimization. Lightweight heads predict an input-adaptive mean and variance, and rollouts sample from this distribution to create alternative latent reasoning trajectories. critic.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model training is mainly at the pretrain–posttrain interface. The paper shows that long-horizon RL need not choose between multi-rollout critic-free optimization and a large standalone critic. If value estimation can be embedded into the causal stream of an autoregressive model, RL post-training for tool use, web interaction, and code execution can become cheaper and easier to integrate into large-scale training pipelines.","technical_approach":"The key idea is not just parameter sharing. SAPO aligns actor-critic learning with the causal structure of autoregressive generation. A value readout is placed at the state boundary before an action is generated, so it only conditions on the current state. The policy then generates the action tokens in the usual causal manner. This lets one backbone represent both policy and value while causal masking enforces the correct information boundary. For optimization, SAPO uses one rollout per prompt, avoids group-relative statistics, estimates advantages with λ-returns, and normalizes advantages at the batch level. Compared with GRPO, it restores explicit value generalization and turn-level credit assignment. Compared with PPO, it removes the need for a separate large critic.","experiments":"The evaluation uses ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B and Qwen2.5-7B backbones. The empirical case is that value-based credit assignment improves long-horizon learning, while the unified causal model reduces the usual actor-critic overhead.","reading_guide":"A 30–45 minute skim is enough. 的表述路径，也可能显著提高后续得到正确答案的概率；反过来，贴近 teacher 的局部输出也未必真的在推进求解。问题不在于 teacher 信号完全无用，而在于它在中间推理步骤上存在局部不可靠性。如果继续把这种不可靠监督均匀施加到整条轨迹，模型会被压回 teacher 的表面轨迹，抑制本来有效但风格不同的推理路径。","importance":"对预训练本身，它不是 recipe 级工作，而是 post-train / reasoning distillation 方向的训练信号校准研究。不过它有两个值得基座研究员关心的点。第一，它把“token 分布对齐”和“过程推进”明确拆开，这对理解 teacher-forced supervision 的局限很有帮助，尤其适用于 reasoning、self-improvement、RL-pretrain 接口等场景。第二，它提出的不是再加一个 reward，而是把 process reward 当作 teacher 信号的可靠性检测器，只在冲突处做过滤。这种思路比直接多目标加权更稳，因为它避免了把高噪声 process reward 直接写进优化目标。贡献量级上更像是对 OPD 训练信号的一次精细修正，而不是新范式；启发主要在“如何使用额外信号校准蒸馏，而不是替代蒸馏”。","technical_approach":"$45","experiments":"全文给出的实验重点不是大规模模型刷新，而是验证“过滤冲突监督”是否稳定优于标准 OPD。任务集中在复杂 reasoning 场景，比较对象包括 vanilla OPD、直接引入 outcome/process reward 的变体，以及不同的 filtering / weighting 设计。主要结论有三类。\n\n第一，标准 OPD 确实存在局部误导。作者通过 case study 和统计分析展示：一些中间段虽然能提高后续 solve probability，但因为与 teacher token 分布偏离较大，会收到更强的蒸馏惩罚；这种 progress–distillation mismatch 在复杂推理题上并不少见。\n\n第二，R²-OPD 的收益来自“删错信号”，而不是“加新信号”。与把 process reward 直接并入优化目标相比，过滤式方法更稳，通常能带来更一致的提升。原因是 raw process reward 噪声较大，直接加权容易把优化带偏；而只在排序冲突时 mask distillation reward，等于只在 teacher 明显不可靠的局部撤销监督。\n\n第三，消融支持了方法中的两个关键部件：sign-consistent merging 和 segment-level averaging。去掉 merging 后，边界噪声会让 conflict detection 更不稳定；改成 token 级直接比较时，局部词汇波动会放大误判。论文还分析了 mask 比例与性能的关系，结论是并非 mask 越多越好，过度过滤会损失 teacher 的有效知识转移。\n\n你给出的全文片段没有包含完整结果表，因此这里不能负责任地填具体参数量、数据规模和精确提升数字。若要补齐数字，优先查实验章节主表、ablation 表，以及 process reward 估计所用 continuation 数和 support size H。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读，不必逐式推导。建议先看 heuristic。\n\n实验部分重点看三类内容：1）标准 OPD 出现 mismatch 的定性案例；2）与直接 reward shaping / reward mixing 的对比；3）去掉 merging、改成 token-level filtering、调整 mask 强度后的消融。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以抓住主要信息。\n\n如果你关心 pretrain ↔ post-train specific failure mode in reasoning-oriented on-policy distillation: OPD implicitly treats token-level agreement with the teacher as a proxy for actual reasoning progress. The full paper argues that these are not the same signal. A student span can improve the probability of eventually solving the problem while still deviating from the teacher’s local trajectory, and a teacher-like span may contribute little to solving the task. The issue is therefore not that teacher supervision is useless, but that it is locally unreliable on intermediate reasoning steps. Applying such supervision uniformly can suppress productive but non-imitative reasoning paths.","importance":"This is not a pretraining recipe paper, but it is relevant to the pretrain/post-train interface because it sharpens the distinction between distribution matching and functional progress. The main contribution is not a new objective, but a calibration mechanism: process rewards are used to test the reliability of teacher-derived distillation signals, not to replace them. That is a useful design pattern. CRS（Caption Refinement Suggester）：从 R_v 的文本侧抽取跨样本一致出现的关键语义元素，作为“应保留/应包含”的提示，约束 caption 不要偏离相似图像的共识语义。\n3) CQA（Caption Quality Assessor）：再用生成的 caption 作为查询做一次检索（caption-to-image 或 caption-to-pair），得到 R_c；比较 R_v 与 R_c 的描述差异，把差异解释为两类错误：\n- 幻觉：caption 引入了导致检索结果偏离 R_v 的内容；\n- 遗漏：caption 缺少使得 R_c 无法对齐 R_v 的关键细节。\n4) 用 CRS+CQA 产出的结构化“修改建议”驱动 RL 训练：不是只给一个 reward 分数，而是引导模型生成新的候选 caption（更像带外部证据的 refinement），从而缓解“RL 只会在原有候选里挑选”的探索瓶颈。\n与常见做法的区别：\n- 不把 CLIP/VLM 当唯一裁判（单标量 reward），而是用检索集合的一致性作为约束；\n- 不依赖额外标注关键词（对比 SC-Captioner），而是从检索到的相似样本中自举出关键语义；\n- 训练信号更接近“可解释的纠错”，降低 reward hacking 空间（但前提是检索库与检索模型本身足够可靠）。","experiments":"给出的全文片段只覆盖到方法开头，缺少实验章节与表格细节，因此无法基于全文核对：具体使用哪些 LVLM（如 LLaVA/BLIP-2/InternVL 等）、哪些 caption 数据集（如 COCO/Flickr30k/NoCaps）、K 的取值、reward 形式（CLIPScore/CIDEr/SPICE 过度贴近检索共识导致“保守描述”的风险。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"RL post-training for image captioning often suffers from reward hacking (e.g., CLIP-based scalar rewards) and weak exploration: the model mostly re-selects high-reward captions from its existing candidate set rather than discovering new captioning strategies. Existing certified robustness methods are limited to single-turn inputs; naive multi-turn composition yields bounds that degrade exponentially in the number of turns. We introduce Multi-Turn Certified Robustness (MTCR), a framework that models conversational safety via State-Adversarial MDPs and defines $k$-turn certified robustness as the worst-case safety probability ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T07:43:06Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20820.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究多轮对话中的安全鲁棒性认证：单轮 jailbreak 防御的认证结果不能直接用于多轮场景，因为逐轮相乘的下界会随轮数快速变小，几轮之后几乎没有可用保证。","importance":"这篇更偏安全认证和理论框架，不是预训练方法论文。对基座研究的价值在于提醒两点。第一，多轮对话的风险不是单轮风险的简单重复，历史状态会积累，攻击者会根据模型回复自适应调整输入。第二，安全后训练或 safety tuning 的评测如果只看单轮，很容易高估真实部署稳定性。它对训练目标设计也有间接启发：如果希望模型在长对话中保持安全行为，仅优化单轮拒答或单轮偏好数据不够，需要显式考虑 safety persistence，即安全状态在多步交互中的保持性质。","technical_approach":"作者把多轮对话建模为 State-Adversarial MDP。对话历史经过表示函数映射成状态，攻击者在每一轮从参考输入附近的 epsilon 扰动集合中选择用户消息，模型按策略生成回复，状态随用户输入和模型回复更新。安全性由状态空间上的 Safe predicate 定义。论文定义 k-turn certified robustness：在任意自适应攻击者下，连续 k 轮保持安全的最坏情况概率。直接用单轮认证概率相乘会得到 p^k 的下界，衰减很快。为缓解这个问题，MTCR 引入两类结构。第一是 embedding-space mode decomposition，把状态空间分解为若干 mode，分别认证 mode 内安全性和 mode 间转移，从而比朴素乘法更紧。第二是 (alpha, beta)-safety persistence，刻画安全状态在后续步骤中持续保持的性质；当 beta 大于最坏单轮下界时，认证退化可从 p^k 改善到 beta^k，并可用 1 - k(1 - beta) 给出更可解释的 horizon 估计。论文还给出非重叠分解下的 tightness 结果，以及缺少结构假设时的 impossibility 结论。","experiments":"实验覆盖六个 production LLM。论文报告 MTCR 给出的 certified bounds 在 epsilon-bounded adversary 设定下成立；在静态 epsilon-ball 攻击和 Crescendo-style adaptive attacks 下，经验安全率都高于认证下界，没有观察到违反 bound 的情况。文中给出一个示例说明朴素乘法的问题：如果每轮认证安全概率为 0.95，则 10 轮下界约为 0.60，20 轮约为 0.36，50 轮约为 0.08；如果要求 k 轮安全概率至少 0.9，只能支持约 2 轮。这组数字解释了为什么需要 compositional bounds 和 safety persistence。需要注意，形式化认证只覆盖 epsilon-bounded perturbations；Crescendo 这类语义级攻击主要作为经验测试，不在严格认证威胁模型内。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–30 分钟。优先看 Problem Formulation，弄清 SA-MDP、k-turn certified robustness 和 naive multiplicative bound。然后看 compositional certification 和 (alpha, beta)-safety persistence 的定理陈述，不必逐行读证明。实验部分重点看六个 production LLM 的认证下界与经验攻击成功率之间的关系。对预训练研究员来说，最有用的部分不是具体认证算法，而是它把多轮安全分解成状态转移、模式结构和安全持久性的方式，可用于思考多轮 safety data、RL 训练信号和长对话评测。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses certified robustness for multi-turn LLM safety. Single-turn certificates do not compose well in conversations: multiplying per-turn lower bounds gives guarantees that decay rapidly with the number of turns.","importance":"This is primarily a safety certification paper rather than a pretraining paper. Its relevance to foundation-model research is indirect but useful. It makes clear that multi-turn safety is a sequential problem: conversation history accumulates adversarial influence, and the attacker can adapt after observing model responses. This has implications for safety tuning and evaluation. A model that looks robust in single-turn refusal tests may not preserve safety over longer interactions, so post-training data and evaluation protocols need to account for safety persistence across turns.","technical_approach":"$4a","experiments":"The evaluation covers six production LLMs. The certified bounds are reported for epsilon-bounded adversaries, and empirical safety remains above the bounds under both static epsilon-ball attacks and Crescendo-style adaptive attacks. A motivating calculation shows why naive composition is weak: with a per-turn certified safety probability of 0.95, the lower bound is about 0.60 at 10 turns, 0.36 at 20 turns, and 0.08 at 50 turns; requiring a guarantee of at least 0.9 would allow only around two turns. The formal guarantee is limited to epsilon-bounded perturbations. Semantic multi-turn attacks such as Crescendo are used empirically, but they are not fully covered by the stated certification model.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. U","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T02:00:21Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20670.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究的是“可弃权行动策略”的 action 脱钩。论文要回答的是，在这种 setting 下，reasoning policy、direct policy、SFT、RL 各自会把能力和可审计性推向哪里。","importance":"它不是预训练论文，但和 pretrain/post-train 接口关系很近，尤其是 reasoning 监督、RL 目标设计、faithfulness 审计。对基座研究员有价值的地方有两点。第一，它把“reasoning 是否因果参与决策”从 QA 场景推进到了 abstention policy，这比普通答案生成更接近 agent 真实部署。第二，它给出一个不太讨喜但很重要的结论：更强的决策器未必更可审计，甚至训练得越像 classifier，reasoning 越可能被压扁成无关输出。这个结论对当前大量把 CoT 当作天然可解释接口的做法是个提醒。就贡献量级看，它更像一篇方法论和审计框架论文，而不是带来新训练范式的工作。","technical_approach":"作者围绕 Qwen3-8B 这个 hybrid model 搭建实验：同一底座既可以用 think mode 先生成 reasoning 再决策，也可以用 no-think mode 直接输出 action token。这样可以在尽量不改模型家族的前提下，对比 reasoning policy 和 direct classifier。任务是二分类 intervention timing：给定多人对话前缀，判断 assistant 此刻是否该插话。数据来自约 1.6 万个合成对话，展开成约 17.3 万个 token-level decision points，其中只有约 13% 是应当 intervention 的正例，因此类别极不平衡。训练上，论文比较了几条路线。其一是直接决策式 SFT，让模型学 action token；这通常能得到更强分类器，但 reasoning 会被抑制。其二是保留模型原生 reasoning 的 masked objective，只在模型本来就能做对的样本上训练 reasoning 相关部分；问题是它拿不到模型做错样本的有效学习信号，因此很难超过 base reasoning policy。其三是 think mode 下的 RL，优化准确率或加入 behavioral faithfulness reward。作者指出 group-relative RL 目标在所有 sampled rollouts 都一致时没有梯度，哪怕这些 rollouts 是一致地错，这会让很多“自信但错误”的 prompt 没有学习信号。审计方面，作者用了 activation probes 和行为干预。activation probe 用来检查决策信息在何时变得可解码；行为干预则通过删除、替换 reasoning 的部分内容，观察 action 是否随之变化，以测试 reasoning 的因果作用。论文还专门批评了一些常见但在该 setting 下会误导的指标：只看概率贡献、忽略类别不平衡的 probe accuracy、只在 reasoning 末尾 probing、以及不受控的 flip test。","experiments":"从全文给出的结论看，最强的 action policy 是不显式生成 reasoning 的 direct classifier；而显式 reasoning 的 policy 更容易审计，但准确率更低，主要损失来自 missed interventions，也就是该说话时没说。这个结果很关键，因为它说明能力和可审计性之间存在实打实的张力。训练实验里，decision-token SFT 会进一步强化 direct policy，但同时让 reasoning 更边缘化；masked objective 因为只在模型原本答对的样本上保留 reasoning 监督，几乎无法把 reasoning policy 推到 base model 之上；think-mode RL 也没有显著超过 base reasoning policy，原因不是 RL 无效，而是 group-relative 目标在 rollout 全同意时不给信号，覆盖不到一批最需要纠正的错误样本。审计实验显示，reasoning policy 的最终决策在 CoT 生成过程中逐步变得可由 probe 解码，而 direct classifier 的决策在读完 prompt 时就已经高度可解码，这支持“后者先做决定，前者至少部分在推理中形成决定”的解释。作者还强调单次训练 run 很容易把 seed variance 误判成 reward 设计差异，因此比较不同 RL objective 时必须做多 seed 或 replicate。摘要截断了具体数值，全文片段里也没有完整主表，但从定性结果和实验设计看，作者更关心机制证据而不是单一 leaderboard 数字。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读偏精一点。先看引言里四条贡献，能快速抓住问题边界：它不是一般的 CoT faithfulness，而是 abstaining action policy faithfulness。然后重点看方法部分关于三类训练目标的定义，以及为什么 group-relative RL 在一致错误样本上没有学习信号。再看审计部分，尤其是 The setting has rare positive actions, asymmetric costs for acting versus abstaining, the possibility that exposing CoT changes the policy being audited, and RL objectives that may further decouple reasoning from action. It first applies monotonic calibration to map confidence scores from different prompts onto a unified scale, treating them as 'soft evidence'. It then models generated candidate answers as categorical states, crucially including an explicit 'null state' (representing the case where all generated answers are wrong). benchma","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T07:11:36Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20794.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论的是：领域 after domain SFT: models may lose the ability to produce factual answers in open-ended closed-book generation even when they can still recognize or rank the correct answer under constrained evaluation. The authors call this factual access failure.","importance":"The useful signal for pretraining research is at the pretrain–posttrain boundary. The paper argues that factual degradation after SFT should not be treated uniformly as knowledge erasure. Some failures are wrong-answer generations, but many are expression-level failures such as verbosity, formatting mismatch, or exact-match artifacts. This distinction matters because erased facts require restoration, while retained but inaccessible facts call for controlling distributional drift during adaptation. The idea is relevant to domain adaptation, continual pretraining, and safety-critical SFT settings where preserving broad factual recall matters.","technical_approach":"The work has a diagnostic part and a mitigation part. The diagnostic compares recognition-style evaluation with open-ended recall. Recognition is measured through multiple-choice or candidate-ranking probes; recall is measured through closed-book generation metrics such as EM and F1. The same-fact probes are important because they hold the underlying fact fixed while changing the elicitation format. The mitigation method, Recall-Anchored Distillation (RAD), adds an unlabeled OOD anchoring stream to the usual supervised target-domain loss. For each OOD prefix-continuation example, the frozen base model provides soft next-token distributions on continuation tokens, and the adapted model is trained to match them. This is a distributional anchor rather than answer replay. It does not require OOD labels, rationales, gold answers, or external judges, and it directly constrains drift from the base model’s open-domain continuation behavior.","experiments":"The experiments fine-tune three backbones on MedMCQA. The matched-fact analysis shows that many facts remain selectable in multiple-choice form but fail under open-ended generation. The failure analysis further separates genuine wrong answers from expression failures. RAD is reported to recover OOD factual generation while preserving in-domain gains and outperforming the compared baselines. We propose \\textbf{C}ontinuous \\textbf{L}at\\textbf{E}nt \\textbf{A}dapter \\textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoi","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T16:36:10Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_21278.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是安全对齐里的一个老问题：安全性一旦通过 SFT、LoRA、RLHF 这类全局参数更新写进模型，模型会把同一套“更保守”的行为施加到所有输入上，结果是 harmful prompt 的拒答能力上去了，但 benign 或 safety-adjacent prompt 也被一并扰动，出现 over-refusal、推理能力下降、事实性变差。CLEAR 的目标不是再做一个更强的安全 adapter，而是把“是否启用安全行为、启用到什么强度”变成输入条件化的连续控制问题。论文把这个问题落在 latent routing 上：冻结 backbone，只训练一个 safety LoRA 和一个很小的 gate，让模型在有害输入上更像安全模型，在无害输入上尽量保持原模型。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发不在“安全 benchmark 又涨了多少”，而在于它把 post-train alignment 里的 safety–utility trade-off 重新表述成条件化参数干预，而不是单一全局策略。这一点对 pretrain/post-train 接口很重要：如果对齐信号本身是稀疏且输入依赖的，那么把它硬写进全部参数往往会污染原有能力；相反，条件化路由更接近 mixture-of-behavior 的思路。CLEAR 还说明了一件实用的事：不需要外部大 guard model，也不需要重训 backbone，只用不到百万级参数的 gate 就能在 HarmBench 和 utility benchmark 上拿到更好的折中。这类方法对持续对齐、可撤销对齐、按风险分层的训练信号注入都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法结构很克制。第一部分是冻结的 backbone LLM；第二部分是 safety-specific LoRA，只负责学习“遇到危险请求时应如何偏向拒答/安全响应”；第三部分是 latent gate，根据输入的隐藏状态表示输出一个连续分数 g∈[0,1]，控制 LoRA 的注入强度。形式上不是简单地在输出端做 rerank 或加一个分类器，而是直接把门控写进层内权重更新：h'=(W+gΔW)h。这样做的意义是，安全干预不是离散开关，而是连续插值，模型可以在‘完全沿用原模型’和‘充分启用安全 adapter’之间平滑过渡。\n\n论文强调 gate 不是外置 moderation 模块，而是和 safety adapter 联合训练。训练上有两个关键设计。其一是 subtype-aware gate weighting：不同类型的 harmful/benign prompt 在训练 gate 时权重不同，目的是让 gate 不只学到粗糙的二分类，而是对安全相邻样本更敏感，减少 benign prompt 被误判为高风险。其二是 hard pairwise margin objective：显式拉开 harmful 与 benign prompt 在 gating space 里的间隔。这个设计很像把安全路由问题做成表示空间上的排序/间隔学习，而不是只靠 token-level generation loss 间接学出来。相比标准 LoRA，区别不在 adapter 本身，而在 adapter 的激活是 input-conditioned；相比外部 guard + base model 的两阶段方案，区别在于 gate 直接控制生成时的内部表示流，而不是先分类再硬切换模型。\n\n从全文表述看，CLEAR 的核心假设是：安全对齐造成的 utility tax，主要来自“对 benign prompt 施加了不必要的参数偏移”。因此它不追求让安全 adapter 本身更温和，而是尽量让 benign prompt 少经过 adapter。这个假设和方法是对齐的，也解释了为什么作者把 backbone 完全冻结：这样 utility preservation 更容易归因到 routing，而不是归因到更好的微调 recipe。","experiments":"实验主线比较清楚：安全看 HarmBench 和 XSTest，效用看 GSM8K、MMLU、TruthfulQA，底座主要是 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 和 Gemma-2-2B-it。结果上，CLEAR 的卖点不是把某一项能力推到极端，而是在几组基线之间拿到更好的 Pareto 点。\n\n在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 上，论文报告 CLEAR 将 HarmBench attack success rate 压到接近 0；与现有安全对齐变体相比，CLEAR 的 ASR 为 0.50%，而其他对齐版本大约在 15.50% 到 24.00% 之间。与此同时，GSM8K 保留到 73.46%，比全局 SFT 和标准 LoRA 高约 7 个百分点。这个数字说明 CLEAR 至少在数学推理这类对安全训练较敏感的任务上，确实减少了 utility tax。\n\n在 Gemma-2-2B-it 上，趋势一致但更能看出小模型受益：GSM8K 从 SFT/LoRA 下的大约 38% 提升到 42%，TruthfulQA MC2 从约 47% 提升到 53%。这说明条件化安全干预对容量更紧张的模型可能更重要，因为小模型更容易被全局安全微调挤占原有能力。\n\nXSTest 的作用主要是看 routing trade-off，即 gate 是否真的学会区分 harmful 与 benign，而不是简单地全局偏高。作者声称用不到 1M 参数的 hidden-state gate 就能得到较好的路由效果，这一点很关键，因为它把 CLEAR 和“再加一个外部安全分类器”区分开了：参数量更小，部署链路更短，也避免了外部模型与主模型失配。\n\n从论文摘要和正文可见的消融重点，应该包括：去掉连续 gate、改成 always-on LoRA；去掉 pairwise margin；去掉 subtype-aware weighting；以及与 SFT、标准 LoRA、已有安全对齐模型的对比。最值得注意的不是绝对分数，而是这些消融是否证明 utility 改善确实来自条件化路由，而不是来自更弱的安全训练强度。如果正文表格里 CLEAR 在相近 ASR 下仍显著保留 GSM8K/TruthfulQA，那这个论点就站得住。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓三件事。第一，看方法图和公式，确认 gate 控制的是层内 LoRA 强度，而不是输出后处理。第二，看训练目标部分，重点找 subtype-aware weighting 和 pairwise margin 是怎么定义的，它们分别解决什么误路由问题。第三，看主结果表，尤其是 HarmBench ASR 与 GSM8K/TruthfulQA 的并列结果，判断它是不是在相近安全水平下保住了效用，而不是单纯放松安全约束。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看 Introduction 里对 alignment tax 的问题设定、方法章节里 gate 的实现与训练目标、以及主实验表和 XSTest 路由分析。预计 20–30 分钟可以形成较完整判断。更值得深挖的点有两个：一是 gate 使用哪一层或哪种 hidden state 作为输入，是否存在层位选择敏感性；二是连续 gate 在生成过程中是 prompt-level 固定、layer-wise 变化，还是 token-wise 动态，这会直接影响它能否处理多轮对话里风险随上下文变化的情况。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"CLEAR targets a specific failure mode in safety alignment: once safety is baked into the model through global updates such as SFT, LoRA, or RLHF-style tuning, the same safer policy is applied to harmful, benign, and merely safety-adjacent prompts. That reduces attack success, but it also perturbs useful behavior on benign inputs, causing over-refusal and utility loss. The paper reframes the problem as conditional intervention rather than stronger global alignment. Instead of asking how to make a safer model everywhere, it asks when and how strongly a safety adapter should be activated for a given input.","importance":"The main value is conceptual and practical. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.   ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-19T18:02:03Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_19338.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的不是某个具体解释算法，而是 mechanistic interpretability 里一个更上游的问题：当我们想恢复模型内部组件的作用、交互和干预效应时，究竟在“测量”什么，测量矩阵如何设计，误差来自哪里，以及什么样的测量结果才足以支持后续控制。作者把 patching、attribution patching、subset intervention、Hessian-vector product 等方法统一写成一个带噪线性测量问题 \\(\\tilde y = Ax + w\\)，把解释问题改写成 tomography，即通过设计干预和观测来恢复目标 effect map。","importance":"对 LLM 基座研究，它不是直接改进预训练性能的论文，但对理解训练后模型内部机制、以及把解释结果用于控制和干预，有方法论价值。重要性主要有三层。第一，它把一批常被分开讨论的 mechanistic interpretability 工具放进同一测量语言里，便于比较它们到底在估计什么，而不是只比较可视化结果。第二，它强调 held-out intervention prediction 和 control-facing validation，而不是只看解释图是否“像那么回事”，这对解释方法的可证伪性更关键。第三，它提出 calibration dimension 这样的概念，用来判断一阶近似何时足够、何时必须显式建模交互项，这对 transformer 中常见的 backup、suppression、self-repair 现象是贴切的。贡献量级更接近统一框架与评估准则，而不是单点算法突破。","technical_approach":"技术主线是把不同解释方法抽象成测量设计问题。这里的 \\(x\\) 不是固定只能表示一个向量；它可以是某种 effect map、局部梯度图、交互矩阵，取决于你选的 basis 和 intervention family。\\(A\\) 的每一行对应一次干预，描述这次干预如何作用到待恢复对象上；\\(\\tilde y\\) 是观测到的响应；\\(w\\) 则统一吸收三类误差：非线性响应、采样误差、basis misspecification。这个写法的好处是，coordinate patching、attribution patching、subset recovery、HVP 都能被放在同一框架下，但它们的 target quantity 并不被强行说成一样。\n\n论文的关键不是停留在形式统一，而是进一步讨论 measurement usefulness。作者分析了在什么条件下目标可恢复、干预尺度和 mask density 如何影响测量条件数，以及何时一阶局部测量必须升级到 interaction-aware 测量。文中提出 calibration dimension：给定一个 baseline map，最小需要多大的修正族，才能让它在有限尺度的 held-out interventions 上预测得足够好。若一个低维修正就够，说明局部线性近似经过简单校正即可用于实际干预；若残差超出该修正族，就该增加交互项、改 basis，或直接换测量方式。\n\n另一个重要点是“observer”概念。作者把能指导干预的估计器称为 observer，并强调 observer 的标准不是重构内部机制有多漂亮，而是它能否在实际使用的干预尺度上预测 intervention effect。这个视角把 interpretability 和 control 接起来：解释若不能预测干预后果，就很难说对控制有用。","experiments":"实验部分主要服务于框架论证，而不是追求某个 benchmark 分数。文中给出的几个关键数字很有代表性。其一，在较大干预尺度下，dimension-one 的简单增益校正可以把 held-out attribution patching 的平均 \\(R^2\\) 从 0.818 提升到 0.960，已经接近 sparse recovery 的 0.969。这说明很多时候一阶图并非完全失效，而是需要一个声明清楚的有限维修正。其二，在 two-HMM wind tunnel 这个控制实验里，闭环 target error 与 observer error 在 10 个 observer 变体上呈现 0.95 的 Spearman 相关，说明解释误差确实会传导到控制误差。其三，作者还做了 specificity test，指出 target improvement 不足以证明 observer 好，因为它可能同时更强地扰动 nuisance state。\n\n这些实验传达的核心信息有两个。第一，held-out intervention prediction 是区分“局部近似还能修”与“必须换模型”的有效标准。第二，控制场景会把解释误差放大成可见后果，因此比单纯的重构指标更严格。就你给出的全文片段看，实验还覆盖了从稀疏恢复到 lifted recovery 的操作性扩展，但没有完整表格细节，因此不适合虚构更多数值。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓三部分。第一是 Introduction 和 Table 1，理解作者如何把 validation，后者给出最直观的数值例子：一维校正为何能显著修复有限尺度干预预测。预计阅读时间 30–40 分钟。\n\n如果你做 LLM 机制分析，值得深挖两个问题。一个是 basis choice：这个框架默认你先选好了组件基底，但现实里 residual stream、head、MLP neuron、feature basis 的选择会直接决定 \\(x\\) two-token starts 出发，统计轨迹最终是否落入 fixed point，并据此定义 fixed-point fraction φ。除了 φ，作者还把轨迹结构分成 funnel、none、fragmented、borderline 等类别，用来描述吸引子几何。关键设计是把 prefix 作为 wrapper live inside the model’s conditional distribution itself, rather than only in task machinery such as instruction following, formatting, or answer extraction? To avoid task confounds, the authors study a task-free deterministic readout: the fixed-point structure of the short-window argmax dynamical system x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t). The core problem is whether prefixes systematically alter this fixed-point structure, and whether that effect can be explained by simple prefix-side factors, model-side factors, or a nearby mechanistic account.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, and its direct impact on training recipes is limited. Its value is more conceptual: it pushes prompt sensitivity below benchmark accuracy and into the geometry of the conditional distribution. If a prefix changes fixed-point structure even when there is no task, no sampling noise, and no verbalizer, then at least part of prompt-model interaction is a property of the model’s internal generation dynamics rather than a benchmark artifact. That is relevant to people studying in-context learning, prefix effects, tokenizer/context interfaces, and mechanistic interpretability. For core pretraining work, it is peripheral but intellectually useful.","technical_approach":"$54","experiments":"The main empirical results are about changes in fixed-point fraction and structural class. The authors report that a nine-token prefix can move the fixed-point fraction across most of its range and can change the four-way structural class, while instruction tuning, despite yielding +60.5 IFEval points on a matched pair, changes the fixed-point class by zero. Another key result is the dependence on window size: the fixed-point structure exists only at short windows, and four of six models lose it entirely as the window grows. The paper then tests prefix-side, model-side, and mechanistic explanations, but none survives sample widening. In particular, attention-sink strength agrees in sign with the fixed-point shift for only 2/5 models, which the authors treat as chance-level support. Overall, the experiments read as one robust phenomenon plus a sequence of negative results: prefixes strongly affect short-window conditional dynamics, but the obvious explanatory decompositions do not hold up.","reading_guide":"This is a skim read. Then read the setup carefully: fixed-point fraction, the four structural classes, the 96-start census, and the window sweep are the core of the paper. In the results, prioritize Sections 3 through 6. First understand the size and selectivity of the effect, then see how the prefix-side, model-side, and mechanistic explanations fail one by one. Read with some caution: this is a highly artificial short-window argmax probe, so extrapolation to normal long-context generation should be conservative. Around 30-40 minutes is enough to capture the main idea. The most interesting takeaway is the asymmetry between short prefixes and instruction tuning: the former can strongly alter this readout, while the latter barely moves it, which may hint at what post-training changes and what it leaves untouched.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"视角很刁钻，剥离了具体任务，纯看短窗口的 argmax 动态，发现指令微调不改变底层的读取结构，值得一看实验设计。","en":"A very unique perspective that strips away specific tasks to purely observe short-window argmax dynamics, finding that instruction tuning doesn't change the underlying readout structure; the experimental design is worth a look."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Independent Researcher","Affiliation:Independent Researcher, Chile"],"authors":["Nicolás Vera Zúñiga"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Prompt Interaction","In-Context Learning","Mechanistic Interpretability","Fixed Points"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21315","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.21315","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.20442","title":"Stored in Optimizer State, Valued by Later Training: A Causal Account of Subliminal Trait Transfer","abstract":"Subliminal trait transfer allows a student model to acquire behavioral dispositions from teacher-generated data in which the trait is not semantically expressed. 优化器动量/二阶矩/时钟等），并给出一个精确分解：transport（物理传播）与 valuation（后续路线赋值）。这会影响三类实践判断：\n1) continual pretrain / mid-train：切换数据域或目标时，优化器状态可能携带不可见的历史偏置；\n2) 对齐与去偏：仅重置权重或仅做数据清洗不一定消除行为残留，可能需要处理 moment buffers；\n3) 是源扰动在移除源数据后继续产生“物理后代（physical descendants）”的主要载体；仅移植一阶矩可在保持当下参数/输出不变的情况下，改变未来更新轨迹；而要恢复终端行为，需要参数 + 一阶矩的组合。","experiments":"从给定正文片段可确认的实验要点：\n- 任务与观测：LLM 实验里终端观测 O 是“目标候选串 vs 参考候选串”的对数似然差，在冻结 prompt It cleanly separates (i) transport: how a perturbation physically propagates through training, from (ii) valuation: how later training and the chosen behavioral readout assign meaning/sign to that trace. 的表征级探针，用来判断数据配比、去偏数据、对齐训练到底改变了内部机制，还是只改变了输出策略。贡献量级不是新的训练方法，而是一个较清晰的因果测量框架。","technical_approach":"方法把职业偏见拆成两个测量点：内部的用户能力表征，以及最终可观察输出。作者先构造“专家”和“新手”两组职业无关提示，例如“我已经深入研究这个主题很多年”和“我刚开始学习这个主题”，用 difference-in-means 得到 expertise steering vector。具体做法是在某一层取隐藏状态，先对 prompt tokens 求平均，再用专家均值减去新手均值得到方向。给定实际上下文时，取上下文最后一个 token 的 hidden state，投影到该 expertise unit vector 上，得到 expertise score。随后用 steering 实验证明这个方向不只是相关探针，而会因果影响行为：在职业问答中改变回答复杂度，在招聘任务中改变是否录用。数据上，作者构造了 20 个常见职业、每个职业 100 个问题的职业问答集，职业来自美国劳工统计局；招聘任务则基于 Karvonen and Marks 的设置，prompt 包含岗位、简历、姓名、经历和教育背景，要求模型给出 Yes/No。","experiments":"关键实验分为两类。第一类验证 expertise vector 的因果作用：沿该方向 steering 会改变职业问答回答的技术复杂度，也会改变招聘任务中的录用率。第二类测量人口属性对内部表征的影响：在只改变人口属性、保持其他内容尽量不变的 prompt pair 中，模型 hidden state 与 expertise direction 的对齐程度会发生显著变化。重要观察是，输出层面的指标，例如阅读难度或回答表面复杂度，并不总是同步暴露这种差异；因此只看行为评测会低估 latent bias。给出的内容没有包含完整模型列表、层选择消融、效应量和显著性数字，精读时需要回到实验表格确认这些细节。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20 到 30 分钟。重点看 Methods 中 expertise vector 的构造、expertise score 的定义，以及 causal model 如何把 demographic attribute、expertise representation 和 output 串起来。实验部分优先看 steering 是否跨任务成立，再看 demographic prompt pair 的控制方式。附录中的 GPT-5 生成职业问题 prompt、招聘任务模板、复杂度指标定义值得扫一遍，因为这些细节会影响结论是否可靠。若只关心预训练诊断，核心是表征偏差和行为偏差不一致这一点。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work asks whether language models still encode occupational competence biases internally even when their observable answers pass behavioral bias tests.","importance":"Its main value for pretraining research is diagnostic. It shows that post-training can suppress biased behavior without necessarily removing the representations learned during pretraining. For base-model teams, this kind of representation-level probe can be useful during pretraining, mid-training, and alignment to separate changes in internal associations from changes in surface response policy. The contribution is not a new training recipe; it is a causal measurement framework for latent bias.","technical_approach":"The method decomposes occupational bias into an internal representation of user competence and an observable output. The authors build an expertise steering vector from two sets of profession-agnostic prompts: expert-like statements such as having studied a topic for years, and novice-like statements such as just starting to learn. At a chosen layer, token representations are averaged per prompt, and the expert mean minus the novice mean defines the expertise direction. For a real context, the expertise score is the scalar projection of the final context-token representation onto the normalized expertise vector. They then test causality by steering along this direction in two settings: a professional QA task and a hiring task. The QA dataset covers 20 frequent occupations from the U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics, with 100 GPT-5-generated questions per occupation. The hiring setup adapts Karvonen and Marks, using prompts with a role, a resume, name, experience, education, and a Yes/No hiring decision.","experiments":"The central empirical result is that the expertise direction mediates behavior. Steering along it changes the technical complexity of answers in the professional QA task and changes hiring rates in the hiring task. The authors then use minimally different prompt pairs to test demographic effects. Holding the rest of the prompt fixed, changing demographic attributes changes how strongly activations align with the expertise direction. A key observation is that surface behavioral metrics, such as reading-level measures, do not always reveal the same differences. This supports the claim that behavioral evaluations can miss latent representational bias. The provided content does not include the full model list, effect sizes, layer ablations, or statistical details, so those numbers should be checked in the experiment tables.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough for most pretraining readers. Focus on the construction of the expertise vector, the definition of the expertise score, and the causal model connecting demographic attributes, competence representation, and output behavior. In the experiments, first check whether steering works across both QA and hiring, then inspect how the demographic prompt pairs are controlled. The appendices with generated QA prompts, hiring templates, and complexity metrics are worth checking because these design choices affect how strongly the conclusions should be read.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看 causal setup 和 steering 验证；它提醒对齐评测只看输出会低估预训练表征里的残余偏差。","en":"Read the causal setup and steering tests; it shows why output-only alignment audits can miss residual bias in pretrained representations."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Boston University","Affiliation:Independent Researcher","Affiliation:Boston University"],"authors":["Keren Fuentes","Aaron Mueller"],"notable_authors":["Aaron Mueller"],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","bias","steering-vectors","alignment-evaluation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20347","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.20347","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.20763","title":"CARD: Diagnosing Belief to Action Routing Failures in Vision Language Models","abstract":"Linear probes and activation steering have uncovered that vision-language models (VLMs) internally represent mental states such as agents' beliefs, knowledge, and intentions. However, it is unclear whether and how these representations are used by downstream predictions along these axes. To close this gap, we introduce Cross-Axis Routing Diagnostic (CARD), which steers activations along one axis while measuring the response of a different axis's prediction. Applied to open-weight VLMs on Relay C","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T05:53:48Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20763.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"CARD 关注一个机制诊断问题：VLM 内部即使能线性解码出伙伴的 belief、knowledge、intent 等心理状态，这些表示是否真的被用于后续 action prediction。传统 probe 只能说明表示存在，same-axis steering 只能说明该表示会影响同一轴的回答；它们无法区分“模型没有表示”与“模型有表示但没有把表示路由到动作决策”。","importance":"这篇更接近 VLM 机制分析和评测方法，对预训练的启发在于区分 representation learning 和 representation use。很多多模态预训练或指令数据会提升模型对状态描述的回答能力，但这不等于模型会在行动、规划或协作决策中使用这些状态。CARD 提供了一个较清楚的诊断框架：用跨轴干预检查信息是否从内部表示流向目标预测。对训练目标设计有参考意义，尤其是 VLM/agentic VLM 中仅训练“说出状态”可能不足，需要构造让状态影响行动的监督或 RL 信号。","technical_approach":"方法分三步。第一，对 belief、intent、knowledge 三个轴分别训练 per-layer、per-head 的 L2 正则 logistic probe，输入是一对互为肯定/否定的 statement，取最终输入 token 的 head activation，得到从负例指向匹配正例的单位方向。第二，选择 held-out accuracy 最高的 top-K=56 个 layer-head 位置，约占全部 heads 的 7%，在 forward hook 中只对最后 token、只对这些 head slice 加入 alpha 倍标准差尺度的 probe direction，alpha 使用 +10 和 -10。第三，不看同一轴输出，而是测量另一个轴的预测变化，例如沿 belief 方向 steering 后观察 action 或 knowledge 的准确率变化。作者用 true-belief 和 false-belief 条件下的 accuracy gap shift，以及 mean-accuracy shift，判断某个内部表示是否被路由到另一个预测轴。Relay Chain 是配套的 cooperative grid-world benchmark，加入了 action axis，用 success 和 counter partition 区分 constant-prior、world-state tracker、belief-conditional 三种行为。","experiments":"实验覆盖四个 open-weight VLM family。关键发现是：belief 表示可以从同一层的 head activation 中线性解码出来，held-out probe accuracy 达到 78% 到 94%；对 belief 方向做 activation steering 后，belief 和 knowledge 预测会出现因果响应，说明这些方向不是纯粹的 probe artifact。但 action prediction 基本不响应 belief steering，表现为 action 相关的 gap-shift 或 mean-accuracy shift 接近零。这个结果指向一种 routing failure：模型编码了合作伙伴的 belief，却没有把该信息稳定用于下一步行动选择。文中还使用正负方向 steering 和 projection-subtraction 作为控制，降低把效果误判为普通输出偏置的风险。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20 到 30 分钟。先看 Method 中 CARD 的定义，尤其是 top-K head selection、alpha=±10 steering、gap-shift 和 mean-accuracy shift 这几个指标。然后看 Relay Chain 的数据构造，确认 success/counter partition 如何区分不同策略。结果部分重点看 3×3 cross-axis matrix：same-axis 是正控制，off-diagonal 才是核心。若只关心预训练启发，可重点关注 belief 可解码但不进入 action 的证据，以及作者如何把这个现象转化为可测的 routing failure。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"CARD asks whether mental-state representations inside VLMs are actually used by downstream predictions. A linear probe can show that a model encodes a partner's belief, knowledge, or intention, and same-axis steering can show that such a direction affects answers on the same axis. Neither test tells whether the belief representation is routed into action prediction. The paper targets this gap: distinguishing absent representations from unused representations.","importance":"The work is mainly a diagnostic and mechanistic evaluation contribution, with a useful implication for multimodal pretraining and agentic VLM training. Improving a model's ability to answer questions about mental states is not the same as making the model use those states when choosing actions. CARD offers a way to test representation use through cross-axis interventions. This is relevant when designing pretraining, SFT, or RL data for cooperative settings: supervision that asks the model to describe beliefs may not train the pathway that turns beliefs into decisions.","technical_approach":"$56","experiments":"The experiments cover four open-weight VLM families. The central result is that belief is linearly decodable from selected head activations with 78% to 94% held-out probe accuracy. Steering along the belief direction causally affects belief and knowledge predictions, which makes the direction more credible than a purely correlational probe. However, action prediction shows little or no response to belief steering: the action-related gap shift and mean-accuracy shift are near zero. This supports the routing-failure interpretation: the model encodes the partner's belief but does not reliably use it when predicting the next cooperative action. The paper also uses opposite-polarity steering and projection-subtraction style controls to reduce the chance that the measured effect is just a generic output bias.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20 to 30 minutes. Start with the CARD method: probe training, top-K head selection, alpha=±10 steering, gap shift, and mean-accuracy shift. Then read the Relay Chain construction to understand why the success and counter partitions are needed. In the results, focus on the 3-by-3 cross-axis matrices. Same-axis entries are positive controls; off-diagonal entries are the main evidence. For pretraining-oriented readers, the most useful part is the evidence that a VLM can encode belief without using it for action, and the way this failure is made measurable through cross-axis activation interventions.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"通过activation steering揭示了VLM内部表征与最终输出之间的断层（有belief但不用），对理解模型内部推理机制和设计更好的对齐/路由策略有启发。","en":"Reveals the disconnect between internal representations and final outputs in VLMs via activation steering (possessing belief but not using it), inspiring understanding of internal reasoning mechanisms and design of better alignment/routing strategies."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Universität Stuttgart","Affiliation:Universität Stuttgart","Affiliation:70569, Stuttgart","Affiliation:Germany"],"authors":["Souptik Kumar Majumdar","Fabian Kögel","Andreas Bulling"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Vision-Language Models","Mechanistic Interpretability","Activation Steering","Theory of Mind"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20763","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.20763","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.20999","title":"Latent Ordinal Evidence, Misaligned Outputs: Inference-Time Ordinal Lens Alignment for Multimodal LLMs","abstract":"Multimodal LLMs apply the language model interface to visual inputs, where ordinal regression tasks such as age estimation, image quality assessment, and disease grading require autoregressive decisions over ordered class labels.
