Across four ordinal benchmarks and four MLLM backbones, ordinal labels are linearly recoverable from hidden states with Spearman correlation up to 0.938, and a task-designe","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-21T11:33:22Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_20999.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个很具体、但对多模态语言模型接口设计很关键的问题：当任务标签天然有序时，MLLM 内部是否已经形成了可线性读出的 ordinal evidence，而这种证据在最终的自回归 digit-token 输出端却没有被正确暴露出来。作者不是在问模型“会不会做年龄分级/病变分级”，而是在拆开看两件事：一是隐藏状态里有没有顺序结构，二是语言模型的 unembedding 和 digit token 子空间是否把这种结构传到了可生成的答案上。全文的核心结论很明确：内部表示里常常已经有很强的有序信息，但原生数字 token 输出接口会系统性丢失这部分信息，因此生成错误并不一定来自表征不足，而可能来自输出读出层与任务结构失配。","importance":"对基座研究的直接启发不在于 ordinal benchmark 本身，而在于它提供了一个很干净的“latent capability vs output interface”诊断案例。很多时候大家把生成错误归因于 backbone 没学会，但这篇文章说明，至少在一类结构化标签任务上，问题可能出在输出几何：hidden state 中可线性恢复的信号，并没有被词表行空间，尤其是 digit-token row space，有效承接。这个观察对预训练和 tokenizer 设计都有延伸意义。它提示两点：第一，LM 接口并不天然适合所有结构化预测；第二，若任务答案被限制在一个很小的 token 子集上，unembedding 的局部几何可能成为瓶颈。贡献量级偏诊断与方法补丁，不是改变预训练范式的工作，但对理解“模型内部会了，接口没说出来”这类现象很有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法分两部分。第一部分是诊断。作者在四个 ordinal 数据集和四个 MLLM backbone 上，对冻结模型的多层 hidden states 做线性 probe，比较不同 prompt 和不同读出位置下，标签是否可恢复。诊断不只停留在 probe accuracy，还进一步分析 unembedding 矩阵对 ordinal direction 的谱过滤，以及 digit-token 子矩阵 W_S 对该方向的能量保留比例。这个分析把“为什么 probe 能读出来而生成读不出来”具体化成了一个输出子空间问题，而不是泛泛地说 decoding 不稳定。第二部分是 OLA。它是一个 frozen-backbone、inference-time 的校正模块，不改 vision encoder、不改 LM backbone、也不改原始 unembedding。做法是在若干中深层 decoder hidden states 上训练轻量 lens，每个 lens 受 digit-token row space 约束，输出一个层级别的 ordinal distribution；再用学习到的 softmax 权重做跨层融合，得到实例相关的 P_OLA。最后只在答案位置、且只在目标 digit token 集合内，对原始 logits 做 discrepancy-driven correction。这里的关键设计有三点：一是用多层而不是单层，因为 ordinal 结构在中深层逐步形成且不同样本最优层不同；二是 lens 锚定到 W_S，而不是自由 probe，因为目标不是证明可读性，而是把可读性接回原生输出接口；三是 correction 只作用于受限 token 集，避免对开放式生成分布造成大范围扰动。与 hidden steering、offline lens 或 LoRA 微调相比，OLA 的定位更像是输出端对齐，而不是重新训练任务能力。","experiments":"诊断结果是全文最有信息量的部分。作者报告，在“task-designed prompt + last-token hidden state”这个最有效设置下，所有 backbone-dataset 组合的线性可恢复性都不低，SRCC 全部达到 0.756 以上，最高到 0.938；文中点名的峰值包括 Qwen3-VL 在 Adience 上 0.938、Gemma-4 在 Aesthetic 上 0.919、Qwen3-VL 在 HCI 上 0.843。与此同时，digit-token row space 对 ordinal direction 的能量保留在全部 16 个 model-dataset 组合里都低于 1.15%，说明输出端确实严重压缩了这类结构化方向。行为层面，hidden-state 线性 probe 与原生 digit-token 输出之间存在 16 到 77 个绝对点的 accuracy gap，这个差距很大，足以支持“问题主要在读出接口”这一论点。方法比较上，OLA 优于 prompt-only、hidden steering、capacity-matched baseline 和 offline-lens 方案，并且在大多数设置下超过了需要 LoRA 调参的 OrderChain。摘要里没有给出完整逐表数字，但从全文叙述看，作者强调的是：在不改 backbone 的前提下，轻量 lens 训练已经能把一部分潜在 ordinal 能力转化为可用输出。若精读，建议重点核对各数据集上的 ACC、MAE、SRCC 三个指标是否一致改善，以及不同层数 K、是否做 W_S anchoring、是否做 target-restricted correction 的消融。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓三处。第一，看 Introduction 和 Task Setup，明确作者不是在做新的 ordinal MLLM，而是在拆 hidden representation 与 output interface 的错位。第二，看 Table 1、Table 2 和 Figure 1，这几处基本给出了全文最关键的证据链：probe 可读、W_S 保留低、行为差距大。第三，看 OLA 方法段，重点理解为什么要做多层 lens 融合，以及为什么 correction 只限制在 digit-token 集合内。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以把主线读清。若想判断它对基座研究有没有更深启发，可以额外看附录里的 per-layer SRCC 曲线和不同 prompt protocol，对“任务提示是否能把内部顺序结构推到最后 token”这个问题会更有感觉。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a precise interface problem in multimodal LLMs: for ordinal tasks, does the model already encode strong ordered evidence in hidden states, while failing to expose it through the native autoregressive digit-token output channel? The key question is not whether MLLMs can solve age grading or disease grading in aggregate, but whether there is a systematic mismatch between latent ordinal structure and the LM output interface. The main finding is clear: ordinal labels are often linearly recoverable from internal representations, yet the unembedding and the digit-token subspace fail to preserve that direction, so generation errors can come from output-side misalignment rather than missing task knowledge.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is diagnostic rather than benchmark-driven. The paper gives a clean example of a broader phenomenon: hidden capability and output behavior can diverge because of readout geometry. That matters for pretraining, tokenizer design, and interface design. If a task is forced through a tiny answer subspace such as digit tokens, the local geometry of the unembedding matrix may become the bottleneck. This does not change the pretraining paradigm, but it sharpens an important lesson: not every structured prediction problem is naturally served by the standard LM interface, and some failures attributed to the backbone may actually come from the output layer.","technical_approach":"$57","experiments":"$58","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on three parts. Third, read the OLA method section with attention to why multi-layer fusion is needed and why the correction is restricted to the digit-token answer set. mode","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-20T06:50:23Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_19701.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是长时程 majority。论文把这个现象定义为 Memory Correlation Bias。核心问题因此不是“如何检索更多 memory”，而是“如何按有效独立证据源而不是表面条目数做仲裁，并在必要时补回缺失的独立证据”。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接相关性较弱。这不是 tokenizer、数据、训练目标、优化或统一建模方向的工作，也不是通用评测资产。它更像是长期记忆型 multi-agent 系统中的推理与检索聚合方法。能带来的启发主要有两点。第一，它把“相关样本重复计数”这个问题讲得比较清楚，这和预训练数据去重、mixture reweighting、synthetic data self-amplification 有结构上的相似性。第二，它强调 query-conditioned dependence，而不是静态 provenance 或 embedding 相似度，这对做 retrieval-augmented memory 系统的人有参考价值。但如果站在基座预训练研究员视角，这篇优先级不高，属于了解问题设定即可。","technical_approach":"方法 CAMA 分成两个部分：相关证据解耦，以及缺失独立证据恢复。\n\n第一部分是 query-conditioned latent-source arbitration。作者不直接把每条 memory 当成独立票，而是把检索到的 memory 映射到若干潜在 evidential factors / latent evidence slots。直觉上，一组 memory 可能都在表达同一个底层证据源，只是由不同 agent、不同时间、不同表述写出来。CAMA 用神经依赖推断去估计 memory 之间在当前 query 下是否冗余，同时引入 provenance-based symbolic priors，利用来源链路、依赖关系、写入轨迹等符号信息帮助判断哪些 memory 可能共享上游来源。然后系统在 latent factor 层面而不是 raw memory 层面聚合对候选 hypothesis 的支持度，从而避免同一证据被重复计票。\n\n第二部分是 sequential evidence recovery。作者认为初始 retrieval 本身就有偏：相似度检索更容易把一簇相关 memory 一起捞出来，却漏掉能打破 false majority 的独立证据。因此 CAMA 不把仲裁限制在初始 top-K 上，而是学习一个逐步恢复策略，在预算约束下决定是扩展检索空间，还是沿 provenance / dependency 链追踪更多 memory，以尽量补齐缺失的独立证据。这个设计比单次检索更合理，因为它承认“当前证据集本身就是偏样本”。\n\n从方法定位看，这不是一个新的 LLM 架构，而是把 probabilistic evidence aggregation、symbolic provenance 和 sequential retrieval policy 组合到 multi-agent memory arbitration 里。创新点在于把相关性建模做成 query-conditioned latent-source reasoning，而不是简单按 agent id、时间戳或语义相似度去去重。","experiments":"实验目标是证明 CAMA 能压制由相关 memory 造成的 false majority，并在多个 long-term multi-agent memory recovery policy 能更稳地恢复正确仲裁。\n\n全文片段没有给出完整表格，因此这里不能准确填写具体 benchmark 名称、提升幅度、恢复步数预算、memory store 规模等数字。按论文叙述，实验还包含两类关键分析：一类是对 query-conditioned dependence 的可视化或案例分析，说明静态属性不足以判断冗余；另一类是 evidence recovery 的消融，验证如果只做相关性解耦、不做缺失证据恢复，性能仍会受初始 retrieval 偏差限制。若要补数字，应查主结果表、recovery budget 消融、以及 provenance prior 去除实验。","reading_guide":"这篇适合快速泛读。先看 Introduction，理解作者定义的 Memory Correlation Bias，以及为什么“多条一致 pretraining，这篇不建议深挖实现细节。更有用的读法是把它当成一个结构类比：相关样本重复计数会导致错误强化，而系统需要估计有效独立信息量，而不是表面频次。这个类比可以迁移到数据去重、self-training 数据污染、memory replay 采样等问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses memory arbitration in long-horizon multi-agent systems, not core LLM pretraining. Its central observation is that existing methods usually treat retrieved memories as independent evidence and aggregate them by voting or weighting. In multi-agent settings that assumption often fails: different memories may originate from the same upstream source, inherit the same bias, or simply restate one another. When such correlated memories are counted repeatedly, the system forms a false majority. The real problem is therefore not just retrieval quality, but how to arbitrate by effective independent evidence sources rather than raw memory count, and how to recover missing independent evidence when the initial retrieval is biased.","importance":"Its direct relevance to foundation-model pretraining is limited. agentic memory 的一个前置失败：在检索开始之前，预算驱动的 eviction 已经删掉了回答所需的上游前提块。检索算法再强，也只能在剩下的 memory store 中排序，无法找回已经被驱逐的证据。","importance":"这项工作对基座预训练本身不是核心贡献，但对长上下文、agent memory 和外部记忆系统有方法启发。它把问题从“怎么检索相关内容”前移到“哪些内容必须先存活下来”。这个区分很重要，因为长程任务中的关键上下文往往不是和当前 query 最相似的块，而是支撑后续事实或决策的前置条件。对于训练具备长期任务能力的模型，类似信号也可以转化为数据构造和评测设计：不仅考最终检索命中，还要考依赖链是否被保留。","technical_approach":"论文先给出 operational definition。memory 被拆成语义原子块，每个块有确定 token cost；benchmark 提供有向 prerequisite graph，边 (j, i) 表示 block j 依赖 block i。给定 query 和预算 C，retention policy 产生一个 block 排序，再按预算贪心保留。失败模式被定义为 structurally indirect prerequisite eviction：某个上游块与 query 语义相似度弱，但位于回答所需依赖链中；如果它在 retention 阶段被挤掉，后续 retrieval 不可能恢复。方法部分提出 Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection，简称 DSGC。它借鉴 tracing garbage collection：先用 query similarity 选出 query-facing root blocks，再沿已知依赖边向上游传播一跳 liveness，保护直接前提；未获得语义支持或结构覆盖的块更容易被回收。和普通 similarity-based eviction 相比，DSGC 的差异在于 ranking 不只看 query-block 相似度，还显式纳入 prerequisite reachability。","experiments":"实验是确定性 benchmark，带固定 seeds、retrieval-friendly controls、dependency-sensitive targets 和 per-seed trace diagnostics。诊断会把失败分解成被驱逐块、挤占预算的竞争块，以及两者分数 margin。主结果很清楚：在 lexical encoder 下，DSGC 将 full-chain retention 从 0.03 提高到 0.90；在 sentence encoder 下，从 0.23 提高到 1.00。这个结果说明，单纯语义相似度排序会系统性丢掉弱相关但结构必要的上游块，而使用显式依赖边可以显著改善完整依赖链保留率。需要注意的是，benchmark 提供 ground-truth prerequisite 和 main table。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper isolates a pre-retrieval failure mode in agent memory. Under a fixed memory budget, an eviction policy may remove an upstream prerequisite before retrieval is called. Once that happens, no retriever can rank or recover the missing evidence because it is no longer in the active store.","importance":"The paper is not a core pretraining contribution, but it is relevant to long-context agents and memory-augmented language models. Its useful contribution is the separation between retention and retrieval. Many systems optimize retrieval quality assuming the evidence is still present. This paper shows that weakly query-aligned but structurally necessary context can be lost earlier. That distinction can inform benchmark design for long-horizon models and training data construction for memory-aware agents: success should include preservation of dependency chains, not only retrieval of semantically similar snippets.","technical_approach":"The setup represents memory as atomic semantic blocks, each with a deterministic token cost. A supplied directed prerequisite graph defines dependencies: an edge from block j to block i means i is an immediate prerequisite of j. For a query and a budget, a retention policy ranks blocks and greedily keeps them until the budget is full. The target failure is structurally indirect prerequisite eviction: a block has low direct similarity to the query but is required by the dependency chain needed to answer it. The proposed method, Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection, uses query similarity to select root blocks, then propagates liveness one hop through declared prerequisite edges. Blocks without direct semantic support or structural coverage are treated as easier to evict. Compared with vanilla similarity-based retention, the key change is that reachability through the prerequisite graph affects what survives.","experiments":"The benchmark is deterministic, with fixed seeds, retrieval-friendly controls, dependency-sensitive targets, and per-seed trace diagnostics. The diagnostics identify the displaced prerequisite, the competing block that displaced it, and the score margin between them. In the main suite, DSGC raises full-chain retention from 0.03 to 0.90 with a lexical encoder, and from 0.23 to 1.00 with a sentence encoder. The result supports the paper’s central claim: semantic retention alone can systematically discard prerequisites that are weakly aligned with the query, while even a simple one-hop dependency rule can preserve the complete chain in this controlled setting. A limitation is also clear from the design: prerequisite edges are supplied as ground truth, so the benchmark tests retention under known structure rather than graph induction.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 15–25 minutes. Start with the operational definition, especially the distinction among retention failure, retrieval failure, and reasoning failure.
